`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/block/FastBreakCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.block;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import dev.weposs.wepac.util.Compat;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * FastBreak / Nuker:
 *  - minimum interval between breaks for blocks that are not instant-break,
 *  - break radius: blocks further than ~4.75 (+ping compensation) from the
 *    eyes cannot be reached (Nuker breaks everything around).
 *
 * Honest-player guards: soft blocks (hardness &lt; 1.0) are ignored, Haste is
 * ignored, and high Efficiency tools insta-mining soft stone is ignored.
 */
public final class FastBreakCheck extends Check {

    public FastBreakCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.FASTBREAK);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;

        Material type = event.getBlock().getType();
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Nuker radius check (applies to every block, including soft ones)
        double dist = player.getEyeLocation().toVector()
                .distance(event.getBlock().getBoundingBox().getCenter());
        double maxDist = 4.75 + Math.min(0.75, player.getPing() / 1000.0);
        if (dist > maxDist) {
            flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                    "broke %s at %.2f blocks (max %.2f)", type.name(), dist, maxDist), 3);
        }

        // instant-break blocks (plants, torches, glass...) and soft blocks are ignored
        if (type.getHardness() < 1.0f) {
            PlayerData.pushTime(data.breakTimes, now, PlayerData.BLOCK_CAP);
            return;
        }
        if (Compat.hasEffect(player, Compat.HASTE)) {
            PlayerData.pushTime(data.breakTimes, now, PlayerData.BLOCK_CAP);
            return;
        }
        // Efficiency V on a netherite/diamond tool insta-mines stone-grade blocks
        ItemStack tool = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
        int efficiency = Compat.enchantLevel(tool, Compat.EFFICIENCY);
        if (efficiency >= 5 && type.getHardness() < 3.0f) {
            PlayerData.pushTime(data.breakTimes, now, PlayerData.BLOCK_CAP);
            return;
        }
        if (efficiency >= 1 && type.getHardness() < 1.5f) {
            PlayerData.pushTime(data.breakTimes, now, PlayerData.BLOCK_CAP);
            return;
        }

        long minInterval = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.FASTBREAK, "min-interval-ms", 90L);
        Long last = data.breakTimes.peekLast();
        PlayerData.pushTime(data.breakTimes, now, PlayerData.BLOCK_CAP);
        if (last == null) return;

        long interval = now - last;
        if (interval < minInterval) {
            flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                    "%s in %dms (min %dms)", type.name(), interval, minInterval));
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/block/FastEatCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.block;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemConsumeEvent;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * FastEat: consuming food/potions faster than vanilla (~1600ms).
 * Dried kelp is excluded - it legitimately eats in ~400ms.
 * Violating consume events are cancelled so the cheat grants no benefit.
 */
public final class FastEatCheck extends Check {

    public FastEatCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.FASTEAT);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onConsumeStart(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        Action action = event.getAction();
        if (action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR && action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) return;
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;

        Material item = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType();
        if (!item.isEdible() || item == Material.DRIED_KELP) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        // keep the earliest pending start; ignore stale ones
        if (data.consumeStart == 0L || now - data.consumeStart > 2500L) {
            data.consumeStart = now;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onConsume(PlayerItemConsumeEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;
        if (event.getItem().getType() == Material.DRIED_KELP) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        long start = data.consumeStart;
        data.consumeStart = 0L;
        if (start == 0L) return;

        long took = System.currentTimeMillis() - start;
        long minMs = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.FASTEAT, "min-consume-ms", 900L);
        if (took < minMs) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                    "consumed %s in %dms (min %dms)",
                    event.getItem().getType().name(), took, minMs), 2);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/block/FastRegenCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.block;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityRegainHealthEvent;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Natural saturation regen fires at most every ~4s in vanilla.
 * Faster regen events indicate a regen hack; violating events are cancelled.
 */
public final class FastRegenCheck extends Check {

    public FastRegenCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.FASTREGEN);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onRegen(EntityRegainHealthEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player)) return;
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;

        EntityRegainHealthEvent.RegainReason reason = event.getRegainReason();
        if (reason != EntityRegainHealthEvent.RegainReason.SATIATED
                && reason != EntityRegainHealthEvent.RegainReason.REGEN) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long minInterval = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.FASTREGEN, "min-interval-ms", 3000L);
        long last = data.lastRegenTime;
        data.lastRegenTime = now;
        if (last == 0L) return;

        long interval = now - last;
        if (interval < minInterval) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                    "regen after %dms (min %dms)", interval, minInterval), 2);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/block/ScaffoldCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.block;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Scaffold: placing a block directly under your feet while looking FORWARD.
 * Legit bridging always involves looking down/back (high pitch) or sneaking.
 */
public final class ScaffoldCheck extends Check {

    public ScaffoldCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.SCAFFOLD);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;

        Block placed = event.getBlockPlaced();
        Location loc = player.getLocation();
        Block belowFeet = loc.clone().add(0, -1, 0).getBlock();

        boolean belowPlayer = placed.getX() == belowFeet.getX()
                && placed.getY() == belowFeet.getY()
                && placed.getZ() == belowFeet.getZ();

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        double maxPitch = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.SCAFFOLD, "max-pitch", 25.0);
        int bufferNeeded = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.SCAFFOLD, "buffer", 4);

        if (belowPlayer && loc.getPitch() < maxPitch && data.lastHorizontalSpeed > 0.08) {
            data.scaffoldBuffer++;
            if (data.scaffoldBuffer >= bufferNeeded) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "block below feet while pitch %.0f, speed %.2f b/t",
                        loc.getPitch(), data.lastHorizontalSpeed), 2);
                data.scaffoldBuffer = 0;
            }
        } else {
            data.scaffoldBuffer = Math.max(0, data.scaffoldBuffer - 1);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/combat/AutoClickerCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.combat;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Click statistics: max CPS over a rolling second, plus "robotic consistency"
 * (suspiciously low standard deviation of click intervals over many samples).
 */
public final class AutoClickerCheck extends Check {

    public AutoClickerCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.AUTOCLICKER);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onClick(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        Action action = event.getAction();
        if (action != Action.LEFT_CLICK_AIR && action != Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK) return;
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        // one physical click can produce both BLOCK and AIR events; dedupe
        if (!data.clickTimes.isEmpty() && now - data.clickTimes.peekLast() < 25L) return;

        PlayerData.pushTime(data.clickTimes, now, PlayerData.CLICK_CAP);

        // Keep a 3-second window so the consistency check can accumulate enough
        // samples. (A 1s window caps at ~40 clicks after dedupe and could never
        // reach min-samples=45, which made the robotic-click detector dead.)
        PlayerData.prune(data.clickTimes, now - 3000L);

        // CPS over the last 1 second (a sub-window of the 3s buffer).
        // 1.9+ note: raw CPS is a WEAK combat signal now - damage is gated by the
        // attack cooldown, and legit butterfly/drag clickers hit 20+ CPS. The main
        // autoclicker signal is robotic interval consistency (below). The CPS cap
        // only catches physically impossible spam.
        int cps = 0;
        for (Long t : data.clickTimes) {
            if (now - t <= 1000L) cps++;
        }
        int maxCps = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.AUTOCLICKER, "max-cps", 25);
        if (cps > maxCps) {
            flag(player, data, cps + " cps > " + maxCps, 3);
        }

        // consistency check over the 3s buffer
        int minSamples = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.AUTOCLICKER, "min-samples", 45);
        double minStd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.AUTOCLICKER, "min-interval-stddev", 12.0);
        List<Long> recent = new ArrayList<>(data.clickTimes);
        if (recent.size() >= minSamples) {
            double[] intervals = new double[recent.size() - 1];
            for (int i = 1; i < recent.size(); i++) {
                intervals[i - 1] = recent.get(i) - recent.get(i - 1);
            }
            double std = PlayerData.stddev(intervals);
            double avg = PlayerData.avg(intervals);
            // humans clicking steadily: avg 80-400ms with wide spread;
            // machines: tight spread around the interval
            if (std < minStd && avg < 400 && avg > 20) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "robotic clicking: stddev %.1fms over %d clicks", std, recent.size()), 4);
                data.clickTimes.clear();
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/combat/CriticalsCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.combat;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import dev.weposs.wepac.util.GroundUtil;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * 1.9+ Criticals detection, two independent signals:
 *
 *  1. Packet GroundSpoof crits: a critical requires being airborne; crit hacks
 *     send onGround=true inside flying packets while falling to force crits on
 *     every hit. The packet claim is compared against server-side physics.
 *
 *  2. Crit rhythm: jumping before every single attack IS legitimate 1.9 play,
 *     but a bot does it perfectly - 95%+ of hits airborne at a metronomic
 *     interval. Humans in a real fight lose the rhythm (strafing, getting hit,
 *     terrain, panic swings), so their airborne ratio and interval spread vary.
 */
public final class CriticalsCheck extends Check {

    public CriticalsCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.CRITICALS);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player attacker)) return;
        if (!enabled() || bypass(attacker)) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(attacker);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        if (data.velocityExempt()) {
            data.critsBuffer = Math.max(0, data.critsBuffer - 1);
            return;
        }

        // server truth: airborne for at least ~0.25s, but the client claims ground
        boolean serverAirborne = !data.serverOnGround && data.airTicks >= 5;
        if (serverAirborne && data.packetSeen && data.lastPacketGround) {
            data.critsBuffer++;
            int needed = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.CRITICALS, "buffer", 3);
            if (data.critsBuffer >= needed) {
                flag(attacker, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "packet onGround=true while airborne %d ticks (forced crits)",
                        data.airTicks), 3);
                data.critsBuffer = 0;
            }
        } else {
            data.critsBuffer = Math.max(0, data.critsBuffer - 1);
        }

        // crit rhythm tracking (PvP hits only)
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player) {
            boolean airborneAtHit = !GroundUtil.isOnGround(attacker);
            PlayerData.push(data.attackAirborne, airborneAtHit, PlayerData.HIT_CAP);
            checkCritRhythm(attacker, data, now);
        }
    }

    private void checkCritRhythm(Player player, PlayerData data, long now) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.CRITICALS, "crit-rhythm", true)) return;
        int minAttacks = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.CRITICALS, "rhythm-min-attacks", 15);
        double minAirRatio = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.CRITICALS, "rhythm-air-ratio", 0.95);
        double maxIntervalStd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.CRITICALS, "rhythm-max-interval-stddev", 60.0);
        if (data.attackAirborne.size() < minAttacks) return;

        List<Boolean> air = new ArrayList<>(data.attackAirborne);
        List<Boolean> win = air.subList(air.size() - minAttacks, air.size());
        int airborne = 0;
        for (boolean b : win) if (b) airborne++;
        double ratio = (double) airborne / minAttacks;

        List<Long> attacks = new ArrayList<>(data.attackTimes);
        if (attacks.size() < minAttacks) return;
        List<Long> recent = attacks.subList(attacks.size() - minAttacks, attacks.size());
        double[] intervals = new double[recent.size() - 1];
        for (int i = 1; i < recent.size(); i++) {
            intervals[i - 1] = recent.get(i) - recent.get(i - 1);
        }
        double intervalStd = PlayerData.stddev(intervals);

        if (ratio >= minAirRatio && intervalStd <= maxIntervalStd) {
            data.critRhythmBuffer++;
            if (data.critRhythmBuffer >= 2) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "robotic crit rhythm: %d/%d hits airborne, interval stddev %.0fms",
                        airborne, minAttacks, intervalStd), 3);
                data.critRhythmBuffer = 0;
                data.attackAirborne.clear();
            }
        } else {
            data.critRhythmBuffer = Math.max(0, data.critRhythmBuffer - 1);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/combat/FastBowCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.combat;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityShootBowEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * FastBow: a fully-forced arrow released faster than physically possible.
 * Vanilla needs ~1100ms to charge a bow; crossbows are excluded entirely
 * (Quick Charge enchantment is legitimate).
 */
public final class FastBowCheck extends Check {

    public FastBowCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.FASTBOW);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onDraw(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;
        if (player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() != Material.BOW) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        // each right-click with a bow is a fresh draw; record its start time.
        // (The old 3s guard ignored re-draws, so a second quick shot measured
        // charge from the FIRST draw and escaped detection.)
        data.bowDrawTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onShoot(EntityShootBowEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player)) return;
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        long draw = data.bowDrawTime;
        data.bowDrawTime = 0L;
        if (draw == 0L) return;

        long chargeMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - draw;
        long minCharge = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.FASTBOW, "min-charge-ms", 300L);
        double minForce = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.FASTBOW, "min-force", 0.75);

        if (chargeMs < minCharge && event.getForce() >= minForce) {
            flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                    "bow charged in %dms with force %.2f (vanilla needs ~1100ms)",
                    chargeMs, event.getForce()), 2);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/combat/KillAuraCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.combat;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerAnimationEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerAnimationType;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Smart KillAura suite. Instead of one naive rule, an ensemble of independent
 * signals is evaluated, each with its own confirmation buffer, so a single
 * lag spike never flags:
 *
 *  A. Rate      - too many attacks per rolling second
 *  B. Angle     - hitting a PLAYER far outside the field of view
 *  C. Snap-aim  - whole rotation onto the victim inside one burst of packets
 *  D. Mouse GCD - real mice quantise pitch deltas to a sensitivity constant;
 *                 packet-set rotations produce arbitrary doubles (GCD ~ 0)
 *  E. Pacing    - metronome-regular attack intervals (stddev below human jitter)
 *  F. NoSwing   - melee damage without an arm swing nearby in time
 *  G. Aim-lock  - crosshair glued to the victim (tiny angle AND tiny variance)
 *  H. Multi     - 4+ distinct victims in 1.5s, each close to the look direction
 */
public final class KillAuraCheck extends Check {

    private static final int ROT_SAMPLES = 40;
    private static final int PITCH_SAMPLES = 25;

    public KillAuraCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.KILLAURA);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Rotation telemetry
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!enabled() || bypass(player)) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        float yaw = to.getYaw();
        float pitch = to.getPitch();
        double dYaw = Math.abs(wrapDegrees(yaw - from.getYaw()));
        double dPitch = Math.abs(pitch - from.getPitch());
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        if (dYaw > 0.001 || dPitch > 0.001) {
            data.lastRotationChange = now;
        }
        data.rotationSamples.addLast(new double[]{now, yaw, pitch});
        while (data.rotationSamples.size() > ROT_SAMPLES) {
            data.rotationSamples.pollFirst();
        }
        if (dPitch > 0.01 && dPitch < 20.0) {
            data.pitchDeltaSamples.addLast(dPitch);
            while (data.pitchDeltaSamples.size() > PITCH_SAMPLES) {
                data.pitchDeltaSamples.pollFirst();
            }
        }
        if (dYaw > 0.05 && dYaw < 50.0) {
            data.yawDeltaSamples.addLast(dYaw);
            while (data.yawDeltaSamples.size() > PITCH_SAMPLES) {
                data.yawDeltaSamples.pollFirst();
            }
        }
        data.prevYaw = from.getYaw();
        data.prevPitch = from.getPitch();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onSwing(PlayerAnimationEvent event) {
        if (event.getAnimationType() != PlayerAnimationType.ARM_SWING) return;
        plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer()).lastArmSwing = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Attack evaluation
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player attacker)) return;
        if (!enabled() || bypass(attacker)) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(attacker);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        PlayerData.pushTime(data.attackTimes, now, PlayerData.ATTACK_CAP);
        decayBuffers(data, now);

        checkRate(attacker, data, now);

        Entity victim = event.getEntity();
        Location eye = attacker.getEyeLocation();
        Vector targetPoint = victim.getLocation().toVector().add(new Vector(0, victim.getHeight() * 0.55, 0));
        Vector toVictim = targetPoint.clone().subtract(eye.toVector());
        double distance = toVictim.length();
        double angle = toVictim.lengthSquared() > 1e-4
                ? Math.toDegrees(angleBetween(eye.getDirection().normalize(), toVictim.normalize()))
                : 0.0;

        checkAngle(attacker, data, victim, angle);
        checkSnapAim(attacker, data, victim, angle, now);
        checkMouseGcd(attacker, data);
        checkMouseGcdYaw(attacker, data);
        checkGuiAttack(attacker, data);
        checkPacing(attacker, data, now);
        checkNoSwing(attacker, data, now);
        checkAimLock(attacker, data, victim, angle, now);
        checkMultiTarget(attacker, data, victim, angle, distance, now);

        PlayerData.push(data.hitAngles, angle, PlayerData.HIT_CAP);
        // 1.9+ meta: attack-cooldown charge at the moment of the hit, for EVERY victim
        // (not just players) so CooldownLock also catches mob-grinding auras.
        // Cooldown-locked bots attack at EXACTLY full charge, every time.
        PlayerData.push(data.attackCharges,
                (double) attacker.getAttackCooldown(), PlayerData.HIT_CAP);
        checkCooldownLock(attacker, data, now);
        if (victim instanceof Player) {
            PlayerData.push(data.hitAnglesPvp, angle, PlayerData.HIT_CAP);
            checkPerfectHits(attacker, data);
        }
    }

    /**
     * J. CooldownLock (1.9+): a cooldown-synced aura fires the instant the weapon
     * reaches full charge - so across many hits the charge value is pinned at
     * ~1.0 with near-zero variance AND the intervals are metronomic. A human
     * fighting for their life swings early, late, and misses the rhythm.
     */
    private void checkCooldownLock(Player player, PlayerData data, long now) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "cooldown-lock", true)) return;
        int minAttacks = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.KILLAURA, "lock-min-attacks", 15);
        double minCharge = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "lock-min-charge", 0.98);
        double maxChargeStd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "lock-max-charge-stddev", 0.012);
        double maxIntervalStd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "lock-max-interval-stddev", 80.0);
        if (data.attackCharges.size() < minAttacks) return;

        List<Double> charges = new ArrayList<>(data.attackCharges);
        List<Double> win = charges.subList(charges.size() - minAttacks, charges.size());
        double[] arr = new double[minAttacks];
        for (int i = 0; i < minAttacks; i++) arr[i] = win.get(i);
        double mean = PlayerData.avg(arr);
        double chargeStd = PlayerData.stddev(arr);

        List<Long> attacks = new ArrayList<>(data.attackTimes);
        if (attacks.size() < minAttacks) return;
        List<Long> recent = attacks.subList(attacks.size() - minAttacks, attacks.size());
        double[] intervals = new double[recent.size() - 1];
        for (int i = 1; i < recent.size(); i++) {
            intervals[i - 1] = recent.get(i) - recent.get(i - 1);
        }
        double intervalStd = PlayerData.stddev(intervals);

        if (mean >= minCharge && chargeStd <= maxChargeStd && intervalStd <= maxIntervalStd) {
            data.cooldownLockBuffer++;
            if (data.cooldownLockBuffer >= 2) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "cooldown-locked: %d attacks at charge %.3f (stddev %.4f), intervals stddev %.0fms",
                        minAttacks, mean, chargeStd, intervalStd), 4);
                data.cooldownLockBuffer = 0;
                data.attackCharges.clear();
            }
        } else {
            data.cooldownLockBuffer = Math.max(0, data.cooldownLockBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    /**
     * I. Perfect hits - the signature of aim-gated auras (Shard-like "attack only
     * when the crosshair settled on the target" gates, raycast-gated attacks).
     * Humans time their clicks, so their hit angles spread widely even at high
     * skill; an aura's hits cluster near zero almost every time.
     * PvP-only: mob farming while staring at a slow target is legitimate.
     */
    private void checkPerfectHits(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "perfect-hits", true)) return;
        int window = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.KILLAURA, "perfect-window", 12);
        double maxAngle = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "perfect-angle", 2.5);
        double minRatio = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "perfect-ratio", 0.8);
        if (data.hitAnglesPvp.size() < window) return;

        List<Double> all = new ArrayList<>(data.hitAnglesPvp);
        List<Double> win = all.subList(all.size() - window, all.size());
        int perfect = 0;
        for (double a : win) {
            if (a < maxAngle) perfect++;
        }
        double ratio = (double) perfect / window;
        if (ratio >= minRatio) {
            data.auraPerfectBuffer++;
            if (data.auraPerfectBuffer >= 2) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "robotic hit precision: %d/%d PvP hits within %.1f deg of crosshair",
                        perfect, window, maxAngle), 4);
                data.auraPerfectBuffer = 0;
                data.hitAnglesPvp.clear();
            }
        } else {
            data.auraPerfectBuffer = Math.max(0, data.auraPerfectBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Time-based buffer decay: isolated suspicious events that are far apart must
     * NOT slowly accumulate into a flag over minutes. If the previous attack was
     * more than {gap}ms ago, this is a separate burst - reset the per-detector
     * confirmation counters so only sustained patterns confirm.
     */
    private void decayBuffers(PlayerData data, long now) {
        long gap = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.KILLAURA, "buffer-reset-gap-ms", 4000L);
        if (data.lastAttackAt != 0L && now - data.lastAttackAt > gap) {
            data.auraSnapBuffer = 0;
            data.auraGcdBuffer = 0;
            data.auraGcdYawBuffer = 0;
            data.auraPacingBuffer = 0;
            data.auraSwingBuffer = 0;
            data.auraLockBuffer = 0;
            data.auraMultiBuffer = 0;
            data.auraPerfectBuffer = 0;
            data.auraAngleBuffer = 0;
            data.auraRateBuffer = 0;
            data.cooldownLockBuffer = 0;
        }
        data.lastAttackAt = now;
    }

    // A. attack rate -----------------------------------------------------

    private void checkRate(Player player, PlayerData data, long now) {
        PlayerData.prune(data.attackTimes, now - 1000L);
        int aps = data.attackTimes.size();
        int maxAps = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.KILLAURA, "max-attacks-per-second", 14);
        if (aps > maxAps) {
            data.auraRateBuffer++;
            if (data.auraRateBuffer >= 2) {
                flag(player, data, aps + " attacks/sec > " + maxAps, 3);
                data.auraRateBuffer = 0;
            }
        } else {
            data.auraRateBuffer = 0;
        }
    }

    // B. field-of-view angle ----------------------------------------------

    private void checkAngle(Player player, PlayerData data, Entity victim, double angle) {
        // players only: sweeping edge legitimately hits nearby mobs off-view
        if (!(victim instanceof Player)) return;
        double maxAngle = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "max-rotation-angle", 75.0);
        if (angle > maxAngle) {
            data.auraAngleBuffer++;
            if (data.auraAngleBuffer >= 2) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "hit %.0f deg off look direction", angle), 2);
                data.auraAngleBuffer = 0;
            }
        } else {
            data.auraAngleBuffer = 0;
        }
    }

    // C. snap-aim ----------------------------------------------------------

    private void checkSnapAim(Player player, PlayerData data, Entity victim, double newAngle, long now) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "snap-aim", true)) return;
        double maxNew = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "snap-new-angle", 10.0);
        double minOld = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "snap-old-angle", 40.0);
        long snapWindowMs = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.KILLAURA, "snap-window-ms", 200L);

        if (newAngle > maxNew) return; // aim not on target - nothing to snap about

        // previous rotation -> angle to the same victim
        Location eye = player.getEyeLocation().clone();
        eye.setYaw((float) data.prevYaw);
        eye.setPitch((float) data.prevPitch);
        Vector toVictim = victim.getLocation().toVector()
                .add(new Vector(0, victim.getHeight() * 0.55, 0))
                .subtract(eye.toVector());
        if (toVictim.lengthSquared() < 1e-4) return;
        double oldAngle = Math.toDegrees(angleBetween(eye.getDirection().normalize(), toVictim.normalize()));

        long sinceRotation = now - data.lastRotationChange;
        if (oldAngle >= minOld && sinceRotation <= snapWindowMs) {
            data.auraSnapBuffer++;
            if (data.auraSnapBuffer >= 2) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "snap-aim: %.0f deg -> %.0f deg in %dms", oldAngle, newAngle, sinceRotation), 3);
                data.auraSnapBuffer = 0;
            }
        } else {
            data.auraSnapBuffer = Math.max(0, data.auraSnapBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    // D. mouse GCD ----------------------------------------------------------

    private void checkMouseGcd(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "gcd-rotations", true)) return;
        if (data.pitchDeltaSamples.size() < 12) return;

        double gcd = 0;
        for (double d : data.pitchDeltaSamples) {
            gcd = gcd == 0 ? d : gcd(gcd, d);
            if (gcd < 1e-7) break;
        }
        double minGcd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "gcd-min", 0.005);
        if (gcd < minGcd) {
            data.auraGcdBuffer++;
            if (data.auraGcdBuffer >= 4) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "rotation gcd %.6f (real mice quantise pitch steps)", gcd), 4);
                data.auraGcdBuffer = 0;
                data.pitchDeltaSamples.clear();
            }
        } else {
            data.auraGcdBuffer = Math.max(0, data.auraGcdBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    // D2. mouse GCD on yaw (auras that only rotate horizontally evade the pitch GCD)
    private void checkMouseGcdYaw(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "gcd-yaw", true)) return;
        if (data.yawDeltaSamples.size() < 12) return;

        double gcd = 0;
        for (double d : data.yawDeltaSamples) {
            gcd = gcd == 0 ? d : gcd(gcd, d);
            if (gcd < 1e-7) break;
        }
        double minGcd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "gcd-min", 0.005);
        if (gcd < minGcd) {
            data.auraGcdYawBuffer++;
            if (data.auraGcdYawBuffer >= 4) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "yaw rotation gcd %.6f (real mice quantise yaw steps too)", gcd), 4);
                data.auraGcdYawBuffer = 0;
                data.yawDeltaSamples.clear();
            }
        } else {
            data.auraGcdYawBuffer = Math.max(0, data.auraGcdYawBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    /**
     * K. GUI attack: in vanilla the mouse is captured by the open inventory -
     * attacking with a GUI open is impossible without mods. Catches auras used
     * while looting/shopping. Buffer guards against exotic plugin GUIs.
     */
    private void checkGuiAttack(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "gui-attacks", true)) return;
        // event-tracked GUI state (InventoryView is version-fragile on Paper 1.21+)
        if (!data.guiOpen) {
            data.guiAttackBuffer = 0;
            return;
        }
        data.guiAttackBuffer++;
        if (data.guiAttackBuffer >= 2) {
            flag(player, data, "attacked with "
                    + (data.guiTypeName.isEmpty() ? "a GUI" : data.guiTypeName)
                    + " open (impossible in vanilla)", 4);
            data.guiAttackBuffer = 0;
        }
    }

    // E. attack pacing -------------------------------------------------------

    private void checkPacing(Player player, PlayerData data, long now) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "attack-pacing", true)) return;
        List<Long> attacks = new ArrayList<>(data.attackTimes);
        attacks.removeIf(t -> t < now - 3000L);
        if (attacks.size() < 8) return;

        double[] intervals = new double[attacks.size() - 1];
        for (int i = 1; i < attacks.size(); i++) {
            intervals[i - 1] = attacks.get(i) - attacks.get(i - 1);
        }
        double std = PlayerData.stddev(intervals);
        double avg = PlayerData.avg(intervals);
        double minStd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "pacing-min-stddev", 25.0);
        // metronome attacks at combat-relevant speed
        if (std < minStd && avg > 40 && avg < 800) {
            data.auraPacingBuffer++;
            if (data.auraPacingBuffer >= 3) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "metronome attacks: interval stddev %.1fms over %d hits", std, attacks.size()), 4);
                data.auraPacingBuffer = 0;
                data.attackTimes.clear();
            }
        } else {
            data.auraPacingBuffer = Math.max(0, data.auraPacingBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    // F. no-swing -------------------------------------------------------------

    private void checkNoSwing(Player player, PlayerData data, long now) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "no-swing", true)) return;
        long window = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.KILLAURA, "swing-window-ms", 400L);
        if (data.lastArmSwing != 0L && Math.abs(now - data.lastArmSwing) <= window) {
            data.auraSwingBuffer = 0;
            return;
        }
        data.auraSwingBuffer++;
        if (data.auraSwingBuffer >= 2) {
            flag(player, data, "melee damage without arm swing", 3);
            data.auraSwingBuffer = 0;
        }
    }

    // G. aim-lock ---------------------------------------------------------------

    private void checkAimLock(Player player, PlayerData data, Entity victim, double currentAngle, long now) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "aim-lock", true)) return;
        if (currentAngle > 5.0) {
            data.auraLockBuffer = 0;
            return;
        }
        Vector targetPoint = victim.getLocation().toVector().add(new Vector(0, victim.getHeight() * 0.55, 0));
        List<Double> angles = new ArrayList<>();
        for (double[] s : data.rotationSamples) {
            if (now - s[0] > 300L) continue;
            Vector dir = directionFrom((float) s[1], (float) s[2]);
            Vector to = targetPoint.clone().subtract(player.getEyeLocation().toVector());
            if (to.lengthSquared() < 1e-4) continue;
            angles.add(Math.toDegrees(angleBetween(dir, to.normalize())));
        }
        if (angles.size() < 4) return;

        double[] arr = new double[angles.size()];
        for (int i = 0; i < angles.size(); i++) arr[i] = angles.get(i);
        double std = PlayerData.stddev(arr);
        double maxSeen = 0;
        for (double a : arr) maxSeen = Math.max(maxSeen, a);

        double lockStd = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "lock-max-stddev", 0.5);
        // crosshair glued to the victim: small angle, near-zero variance
        if (std < lockStd && maxSeen < 6.0) {
            data.auraLockBuffer++;
            if (data.auraLockBuffer >= 3) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "pixel-perfect aim lock: angle stddev %.2f deg over %d samples", std, arr.length), 3);
                data.auraLockBuffer = 0;
            }
        } else {
            data.auraLockBuffer = Math.max(0, data.auraLockBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    // H. multi-target ---------------------------------------------------------------

    private void checkMultiTarget(Player player, PlayerData data, Entity victim,
                                  double angle, double distance, long now) {
        if (!plugin.config().optBool(CheckType.KILLAURA, "multi-target", true)) return;
        long window = plugin.config().optLongOr(CheckType.KILLAURA, "multi-window-ms", 1500L);
        int maxTargets = plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.KILLAURA, "multi-max-targets", 4);
        double maxAngle = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.KILLAURA, "multi-max-angle", 40.0);

        UUID id = victim.getUniqueId();
        data.recentTargets.put(id, new double[]{now, angle});
        data.recentTargets.values().removeIf(v -> now - v[0] > window);

        // only count victims that were close to the look direction:
        // sweeping edge hits mobs off-view and must not count
        int onView = 0;
        for (double[] v : data.recentTargets.values()) {
            if (v[1] <= maxAngle) onView++;
        }
        if (onView >= maxTargets) {
            data.auraMultiBuffer++;
            if (data.auraMultiBuffer >= 2) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "%d distinct victims in %.1fs (reach up to %.1f)",
                        onView, window / 1000.0, distance), 3);
                data.auraMultiBuffer = 0;
                data.recentTargets.clear();
            }
        } else {
            data.auraMultiBuffer = Math.max(0, data.auraMultiBuffer - 1);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static double gcd(double a, double b) {
        a = Math.abs(a);
        b = Math.abs(b);
        while (b > 1e-7) {
            double t = a % b;
            a = b;
            b = t;
        }
        return a;
    }

    private static Vector directionFrom(float yaw, float pitch) {
        double pitchRad = Math.toRadians(pitch);
        double yawRad = Math.toRadians(yaw);
        double xz = Math.cos(pitchRad);
        return new Vector(-xz * Math.sin(yawRad), -Math.sin(pitchRad), xz * Math.cos(yawRad)).normalize();
    }

    private static double angleBetween(Vector a, Vector b) {
        double dot = Math.max(-1.0, Math.min(1.0, a.dot(b)));
        return Math.acos(dot);
    }

    private static float wrapDegrees(float deg) {
        float d = deg % 360.0f;
        if (d >= 180.0f) d -= 360.0f;
        if (d < -180.0f) d += 360.0f;
        return d;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/combat/ReachCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.combat;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Lag-compensated Reach (v2).
 *
 * The attacker's client sees the victim where it was up to (ping) milliseconds ago.
 * A 20Hz position history is kept for every player; on each hit we measure the
 * eye-to-hitbox distance against the victim's positions inside the window the
 * attacker could legitimately have seen, and take the MINIMUM. Only if the hit
 * exceeds max range against every candidate position is it a violation.
 * This removes the classic high-ping false positives and still catches real
 * reach, because a reach hack extends distance at every point in time.
 */
public final class ReachCheck extends Check {

    public ReachCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.REACH);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player attacker)) return;
        if (!enabled() || bypass(attacker)) return;

        Entity victim = event.getEntity();
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(attacker);

        double maxReach = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.REACH, "max-reach", 3.05);
        double pingComp = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.REACH, "ping-compensation", 0.35);
        double maxComp = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.REACH, "max-ping-compensation", 0.6);
        double allowed = maxReach
                + Math.min(maxComp, attacker.getPing() / 1000.0 * pingComp)
                + (attacker.getVelocity().length() > 0.1 ? 0.35 : 0.0);

        Location eye = attacker.getEyeLocation();
        Vector eyeVec = eye.toVector();
        Vector dir = eye.getDirection();

        // distance against the victim's CURRENT position
        double minDistance = rayOrBox(eyeVec, dir, victim.getBoundingBox());

        // lag compensation: the attacker saw the victim in the recent past
        int vPing = victim instanceof Player ? ((Player) victim).getPing() : 0;
        long windowStart = System.currentTimeMillis()
                - Math.min(600L, attacker.getPing() + vPing / 2L + 150L);
        PlayerData victimData = plugin.data().getIfLoaded(victim.getUniqueId());
        if (victimData != null) {
            double w = victim.getWidth();
            double h = victim.getHeight();
            for (double[] pos : victimData.posHistory) {
                if (pos[0] < windowStart) continue;
                BoundingBox box = BoundingBox.of(
                        new Vector(pos[1], pos[2] + h / 2.0, pos[3]), w, h, w);
                minDistance = Math.min(minDistance, rayOrBox(eyeVec, dir, box));
            }
        }

        PlayerData.push(data.reachSamples, Math.min(minDistance, 8.0), PlayerData.REACH_CAP);

        if (minDistance > allowed) {
            flag(attacker, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                    "%.2f > %.2f blocks (lag-compensated)", minDistance, allowed), 2);
        }
    }

    private static double rayOrBox(Vector eye, Vector dir, BoundingBox box) {
        RayTraceResult hit = box.rayTrace(eye, dir, 8.0);
        return hit != null ? hit.getHitPosition().distance(eye) : distanceToBox(eye, box);
    }

    private static double distanceToBox(Vector point, BoundingBox box) {
        double dx = Math.max(box.getMinX() - point.getX(),
                Math.max(0, point.getX() - box.getMaxX()));
        double dy = Math.max(box.getMinY() - point.getY(),
                Math.max(0, point.getY() - box.getMaxY()));
        double dz = Math.max(box.getMinZ() - point.getZ(),
                Math.max(0, point.getZ() - box.getMaxZ()));
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }
}
```

```java
    }
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/checks/movement/BoatFlyCheck.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.checks.movement;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Boat;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleDestroyEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleExitEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleMoveEvent;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * BoatFly (CatLean BoatFly.kt, KeyzDLC BoatFly side=0.6 up=0.4, Zenith/Winlend/
 * Xrose boat modes): boats obey gravity and float on water - they cannot hover,
 * climb, or exceed rowing speed. Physics is evaluated on every VehicleMoveEvent
 * with per-signal streak buffers.
 *
 * False-positive guards: bubble columns and waterfalls (in-water exempt),
 * launches/explosions (velocity grace), ballistic flight (falling = not hovering),
 * ice boat roads (separate generous cap).
 *
 * Punishment: threshold 3 + severity 5 per confirmed flag + the BOATFLY stage
 * override means an instant tempban on the first confirmed violation - the
 * signal is deterministic, a boat in the sky for 0.3s has no honest explanation.
 */
public final class BoatFlyCheck extends Check {

    // boat uuid -> [hoverStreak, riseStreak, speedStreak]
    private final Map<UUID, int[]> streaks = new HashMap<>();

    public BoatFlyCheck(WepAC plugin) {
        super(plugin, CheckType.BOATFLY);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVehicleMove(VehicleMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled()) return;
        Entity vehicle = event.getVehicle();
        if (!(vehicle instanceof Boat)) return;
        // snapshot the passenger list once: a passenger could exit between the
        // isEmpty() check and get(0), which would throw IndexOutOfBoundsException
        java.util.List<Entity> passengers = vehicle.getPassengers();
        if (passengers.isEmpty()) return;
        if (!(passengers.get(0) instanceof Player player)) return;
        if (player.hasPermission("wepac.bypass")) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        double dx = to.getX() - from.getX();
        double dy = to.getY() - from.getY();
        double dz = to.getZ() - from.getZ();
        double hDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);

        int[] st = streaks.computeIfAbsent(vehicle.getUniqueId(), k -> new int[3]);
        if (streaks.size() > 1000) streaks.clear(); // safety valve

        // just launched (explosion, collision, drop) - grace
        if (vehicle.getVelocity().lengthSquared() > 0.12) {
            st[0] = 0;
            st[1] = 0;
            st[2] = 0;
            return;
        }

        Block at = to.getBlock();
        Block below = to.clone().add(0, -0.3, 0).getBlock();
        Material atType = at.getType();
        Material belowType = below.getType();

        boolean inWater = atType == Material.WATER || belowType == Material.WATER;
        boolean bubble = atType == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN || belowType == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN;
        boolean onIce = belowType.name().contains("ICE");
        boolean onGround = belowType.isSolid() && !inWater && !bubble;
        boolean airborne = !inWater && !bubble && !onGround;

        // A) hovering / flying: airborne and NOT falling (gravity always wins)
        if (airborne && dy > -0.04) {
            st[0]++;
            if (st[0] >= plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.BOATFLY, "hover-events", 6)) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "boat hovering %d events at y=%.1f (boats obey gravity)", st[0], to.getY()), 5);
                st[0] = 0;
            }
        } else {
            st[0] = Math.max(0, st[0] - 1);
        }

        // B) powered climb outside water (bubble columns legitimately lift boats)
        if (!inWater && !bubble && dy > 0.05) {
            st[1]++;
            if (st[1] >= plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.BOATFLY, "rise-events", 4)) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "boat climbing +%.2f/event out of water", dy), 5);
                st[1] = 0;
            }
        } else {
            st[1] = Math.max(0, st[1] - 1);
        }

        // C) speed caps per surface (rowing ~0.4 b/t, ice roads up to ~1.6)
        double cap;
        String surface;
        if (onIce) {
            cap = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.BOATFLY, "ice-speed-cap", 1.8);
            surface = "ice";
        } else if (inWater) {
            cap = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.BOATFLY, "water-speed-cap", 0.55);
            surface = "water";
        } else {
            cap = plugin.config().optDouble(CheckType.BOATFLY, "land-speed-cap", 0.35);
            surface = "land";
        }
        if (hDist > cap) {
            st[2]++;
            if (st[2] >= plugin.config().optInt(CheckType.BOATFLY, "speed-events", 8)) {
                flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "boat speed %.2f b/t > %.2f on %s", hDist, cap, surface), 5);
                st[2] = 0;
            }
        } else {
            st[2] = Math.max(0, st[2] - 1);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onExit(VehicleExitEvent event) {
        streaks.remove(event.getVehicle().getUniqueId());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onDestroy(VehicleDestroyEvent event) {
        streaks.remove(event.getVehicle().getUniqueId());
    }
}

```
