`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/config/ConfigManager.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.config;

import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import org.bukkit.ChatColor;
import org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/** Typed view over config.yml with reload support. */
public final class ConfigManager {

    public static final class PunishStage {
        public final int at;
        public final String command;

        public PunishStage(int at, String command) {
            this.at = at;
            this.command = command;
        }
    }

    private final JavaPlugin plugin;

    private String prefix;
    private String alertFormat;
    private boolean alertConsole;

    private int decayInterval;
    private int decayAmount;

    // false-positive guards
    public boolean lagGuard;
    public int lagPingThreshold;
    public boolean rollbackEnabled;

    // kick/ban confirmation
    public boolean confirmEnabled;
    public int confirmMinDistinctChecks;
    public double confirmSingleVlMultiplier;
    public double confirmAiProbability;

    // police record ("учёт")
    public boolean recordEnabled;
    public String recordFile;
    public double watchMultiplier;
    public double wantedMultiplier;
    public double reviewBanScore;
    public String recordBanCommand;
    public String rageMessage;
    public int rageRepeat;

    // client detection
    public boolean clientDetectEnabled;
    public boolean clientNotifyStaff;
    public boolean clientWatchUnknown;
    public String clientBanCommand;
    public List<String> cheatBrands = new ArrayList<>();
    public List<String> watchBrands = new ArrayList<>();
    public Map<String, String> knownBrands = new HashMap<>();

    // packet layer
    public boolean packetEnabled;
    public int timerMaxRate;
    public long timerSustainMs;
    public long blinkTimeoutMs;

    private boolean punishmentsEnabled;
    private List<PunishStage> defaultStages;
    private Map<CheckType, List<PunishStage>> stageOverrides;

    // AI
    public boolean aiEnabled;
    public double aiLearningRate;
    public int aiBatchSize;
    public int aiTrainInterval;
    public int aiScoreInterval;
    public int aiSaveIntervalMinutes;
    public String aiModelFile;
    public boolean aiLabelFromPunishments;
    public boolean aiLabelFromCleanPlay;
    public int aiCleanPlayMinutes;
    public int aiMaxCleanSamples;
    public double aiAlertThreshold;
    public boolean aiAdaptiveThresholds;
    public double aiAdaptiveMin;
    public double aiAdaptiveMax;
    public boolean aiPunishByAi;
    public double aiPunishProbability;

    // learning from real PvP ("how honest players fight")
    public boolean pvpLearningEnabled;
    public int pvpSessionTimeoutSeconds;
    public int pvpMinSessionSeconds;
    public int pvpMinHits;
    public double pvpMaxAiProbability;
    public int pvpMaxSamplesPerHour;

    // Checks
    private final Map<CheckType, Boolean> enabled = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<CheckType, Integer> thresholds = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, Object> options = new HashMap<>();

    public ConfigManager(JavaPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void load() {
        plugin.saveDefaultConfig();
        plugin.reloadConfig();
        FileConfiguration c = plugin.getConfig();

        prefix = color(c.getString("prefix", "&8[&cWepAC&8]&r "));
        alertFormat = color(c.getString("alerts.format",
                "{prefix}&7{player} &8| &c{check} &8| &7VL {vl} &8| &7ping {ping}ms &8| &7AI {ai} &8| &f{info}"));
        alertConsole = c.getBoolean("alerts.console", true);

        decayInterval = Math.max(5, c.getInt("decay.interval", 30));
        decayAmount = Math.max(1, c.getInt("decay.amount", 1));

        lagGuard = c.getBoolean("guards.lag-guard", true);
        lagPingThreshold = c.getInt("guards.lag-ping-threshold", 450);
        rollbackEnabled = c.getBoolean("guards.rollback", true);

        confirmEnabled = c.getBoolean("confirm.enabled", true);
        confirmMinDistinctChecks = Math.max(1, c.getInt("confirm.min-distinct-checks", 2));
        confirmSingleVlMultiplier = c.getDouble("confirm.single-check-vl-multiplier", 1.5);
        confirmAiProbability = c.getDouble("confirm.ai-probability", 0.97);

        recordEnabled = c.getBoolean("record.enabled", true);
        recordFile = c.getString("record.file", "records.yml");
        watchMultiplier = c.getDouble("record.watch-threshold-multiplier", 0.6);
        wantedMultiplier = c.getDouble("record.wanted-threshold-multiplier", 0.4);
        reviewBanScore = c.getDouble("record.review-ban-score", 100);
        recordBanCommand = c.getString("record.ban-command",
                "tempban %player% 7d &c[WepAC] Рецидив после учёта (%check%)");
        rageMessage = c.getString("record.rage-message",
                "&4&l%player%&c&l, ЧИТАК ЕБУЧИЙ СУКА БАНЬТЕ БЛЯТЬ БЫСТРЕЕ НАХУЙ");
        rageRepeat = Math.max(1, c.getInt("record.rage-repeat", 3));

        clientDetectEnabled = c.getBoolean("client-detect.enabled", true);
        clientNotifyStaff = c.getBoolean("client-detect.notify-staff", true);
        clientWatchUnknown = c.getBoolean("client-detect.watch-unknown", true);
        clientBanCommand = c.getString("client-detect.ban-command",
                "ban %player% &c[WepAC] Читерский клиент: %client%");
        cheatBrands = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String s : c.getStringList("client-detect.cheat-brands")) {
            cheatBrands.add(s.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).trim());
        }
        watchBrands = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String s : c.getStringList("client-detect.watch-brands")) {
            watchBrands.add(s.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).trim());
        }
        knownBrands = new HashMap<>();
        ConfigurationSection known = c.getConfigurationSection("client-detect.known-brands");
        if (known != null) {
            for (String key : known.getKeys(false)) {
                knownBrands.put(key.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).trim(), known.getString(key, key));
            }
        }

        packetEnabled = c.getBoolean("packet-sniffer.enabled", true);
        timerMaxRate = c.getInt("packet-sniffer.timer-max-rate", 21);
        timerSustainMs = c.getLong("packet-sniffer.timer-sustain-ms", 2500L);
        blinkTimeoutMs = c.getLong("packet-sniffer.blink-timeout-ms", 2000L);

        punishmentsEnabled = c.getBoolean("punishments.enabled", true);
        defaultStages = readStages(c.getMapList("punishments.default-stages"));
        stageOverrides = new HashMap<>();
        ConfigurationSection overrides = c.getConfigurationSection("punishments.overrides");
        if (overrides != null) {
            for (String key : overrides.getKeys(false)) {
                try {
                    CheckType type = CheckType.valueOf(key.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
                    stageOverrides.put(type,
                            readStages(c.getMapList("punishments.overrides." + key)));
                } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                    plugin.getLogger().warning("Unknown check in punishments.overrides: " + key);
                }
            }
        }

        ConfigurationSection ai = c.getConfigurationSection("ai");
        aiEnabled = c.getBoolean("ai.enabled", true);
        aiLearningRate = ai != null ? ai.getDouble("learning-rate", 0.02) : 0.02;
        aiBatchSize = ai != null ? ai.getInt("batch-size", 32) : 32;
        aiTrainInterval = ai != null ? Math.max(10, ai.getInt("train-interval", 60)) : 60;
        aiScoreInterval = ai != null ? Math.max(1, ai.getInt("score-interval", 3)) : 3;
        aiSaveIntervalMinutes = ai != null ? Math.max(1, ai.getInt("save-interval-minutes", 5)) : 5;
        aiModelFile = ai != null ? ai.getString("model-file", "ai/model.bin") : "ai/model.bin";
        aiLabelFromPunishments = c.getBoolean("ai.auto-label.from-punishments", true);
        aiLabelFromCleanPlay = c.getBoolean("ai.auto-label.from-clean-play", true);
        aiCleanPlayMinutes = Math.max(1, c.getInt("ai.auto-label.clean-play-minutes", 20));
        aiMaxCleanSamples = Math.max(1, c.getInt("ai.auto-label.max-clean-samples", 3));
        aiAlertThreshold = c.getDouble("ai.alert-threshold", 0.90);
        aiAdaptiveThresholds = c.getBoolean("ai.adaptive-thresholds", true);
        aiAdaptiveMin = c.getDouble("ai.adaptive-min-multiplier", 0.65);
        aiAdaptiveMax = c.getDouble("ai.adaptive-max-multiplier", 1.30);
        aiPunishByAi = c.getBoolean("ai.punish-by-ai", false);
        aiPunishProbability = c.getDouble("ai.punish-probability", 0.995);

        pvpLearningEnabled = c.getBoolean("ai.pvp-learning.enabled", true);
        pvpSessionTimeoutSeconds = Math.max(3, c.getInt("ai.pvp-learning.session-timeout-seconds", 10));
        pvpMinSessionSeconds = Math.max(5, c.getInt("ai.pvp-learning.min-session-seconds", 15));
        pvpMinHits = Math.max(4, c.getInt("ai.pvp-learning.min-hits", 20));
        pvpMaxAiProbability = c.getDouble("ai.pvp-learning.max-ai-probability", 0.35);
        pvpMaxSamplesPerHour = Math.max(1, c.getInt("ai.pvp-learning.max-samples-per-hour", 3));

        enabled.clear();
        thresholds.clear();
        options.clear();
        ConfigurationSection checks = c.getConfigurationSection("checks");
        if (checks != null) {
            for (CheckType type : CheckType.values()) {
                String key = type.configKey();
                if (type == CheckType.AI || type == CheckType.GROUND_SPOOF) {
                    enabled.put(type, true);
                    thresholds.put(type, 30);
                }
                enabled.put(type, checks.getBoolean(key + ".enabled", true));
                thresholds.put(type, Math.max(1, checks.getInt(key + ".threshold", 20)));
                ConfigurationSection sec = checks.getConfigurationSection(key);
                if (sec != null) {
                    for (String opt : sec.getKeys(false)) {
                        if (!opt.equals("enabled") && !opt.equals("threshold")) {
                            options.put(key + "." + opt, sec.get(opt));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            for (CheckType type : CheckType.values()) {
                enabled.put(type, true);
                thresholds.put(type, 20);
            }
        }
    }

    private List<PunishStage> readStages(List<Map<?, ?>> list) {
        List<PunishStage> stages = new ArrayList<>();
        if (list == null) return stages;
        for (Map<?, ?> map : list) {
            Object cmd = map.get("command");
            stages.add(new PunishStage(
                    toInt(map.get("at"), Integer.MAX_VALUE),
                    cmd == null ? "" : String.valueOf(cmd)));
        }
        stages.sort((a, b) -> Integer.compare(a.at, b.at));
        return stages;
    }

    private static int toInt(Object o, int def) {
        return o instanceof Number ? ((Number) o).intValue() : def;
    }

    public boolean isEnabled(CheckType type) {
        return enabled.getOrDefault(type, true);
    }

    public int threshold(CheckType type) {
        return thresholds.getOrDefault(type, 20);
    }

    public List<PunishStage> stages(CheckType type) {
        return stageOverrides.getOrDefault(type, defaultStages);
    }

    public String prefix() {
        return prefix;
    }

    public String alertFormat() {
        return alertFormat;
    }

    public boolean alertConsole() {
        return alertConsole;
    }

    public int decayInterval() {
        return decayInterval;
    }

    public int decayAmount() {
        return decayAmount;
    }

    public boolean punishmentsEnabled() {
        return punishmentsEnabled;
    }

    public double optDouble(CheckType type, String key, double def) {
        Object v = options.get(type.configKey() + "." + key);
        return v instanceof Number ? ((Number) v).doubleValue() : def;
    }

    public int optInt(CheckType type, String key, int def) {
        Object v = options.get(type.configKey() + "." + key);
        return v instanceof Number ? ((Number) v).intValue() : def;
    }

    public boolean optBool(CheckType type, String key, boolean def) {
        Object v = options.get(type.configKey() + "." + key);
        return v instanceof Boolean ? (Boolean) v : def;
    }

    public long optLongOr(CheckType type, String key, long def) {
        Object v = options.get(type.configKey() + "." + key);
        return v instanceof Number ? ((Number) v).longValue() : def;
    }

    public static String color(String s) {
        return ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', s);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/data/DataManager.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.data;

import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class DataManager {

    private final Map<UUID, PlayerData> data = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public PlayerData get(Player player) {
        return data.computeIfAbsent(player.getUniqueId(),
                id -> new PlayerData(id, player.getName()));
    }

    public PlayerData getIfLoaded(UUID uuid) {
        return data.get(uuid);
    }

    public Collection<PlayerData> all() {
        return data.values();
    }

    public void remove(UUID uuid) {
        data.remove(uuid);
    }

    public void clear() {
        data.clear();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/data/PlayerData.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.data;

import dev.weposs.wepac.ai.PlayerProfile;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import org.bukkit.Location;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/** Rolling telemetry + violation state for one player. All access on main thread. */
public final class PlayerData {

    public static final int CLICK_CAP = 120;
    public static final int ATTACK_CAP = 60;
    public static final int REACH_CAP = 20;
    public static final int SPEED_CAP = 60;
    public static final int AIR_CAP = 100;
    public static final int ROT_CAP = 60;
    public static final int BLOCK_CAP = 40;
    public static final int HIT_CAP = 20;

    public final UUID uuid;
    public String name;
    public long joinTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

    // ---- client detection ----
    public String clientBrand = "?";
    public String clientVerdict = "?";   // CHEAT / SUSPICIOUS / KNOWN / UNKNOWN
    public String clientName = "";       // pretty display name for known clients

    // ---- movement ----
    public boolean serverOnGround = true;
    public int airTicks = 0;
    public int hoverTicks = 0;
    public int jesusTicks = 0;
    public long velocityExemptUntil = 0L;
    public long lastTeleport = 0L;
    public long lastRespawn = 0L;
    public Location lastLocation;
    public Location lastGroundLocation;
    public boolean prevAirborne = false;
    public int phaseTicks = 0;
    public int airJumpBuffer = 0;
    public double lastHorizontalSpeed = 0.0;
    public double lastVerticalSpeed = 0.0;
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> speedSamples = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public final ArrayDeque<Boolean> airborneSamples = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public final ArrayDeque<Boolean> risingSamples = new ArrayDeque<>();

    // ---- combat ----
    public final ArrayDeque<Long> clickTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public final ArrayDeque<Long> attackTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> reachSamples = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> yawDeltas = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> pitchDeltas = new ArrayDeque<>();
    /** aim-to-victim angle (deg) at the moment of each hit, all victims */
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> hitAngles = new ArrayDeque<>();
    /** same, but PvP hits only (perfect-hits detector) */
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> hitAnglesPvp = new ArrayDeque<>();
    /** 1.9+ attack cooldown charge (0..1) at the moment of each PvP hit */
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> attackCharges = new ArrayDeque<>();
    /** was the attacker airborne (server truth) at each PvP hit - crit rhythm */
    public final ArrayDeque<Boolean> attackAirborne = new ArrayDeque<>();

    // ---- killaura telemetry ----
    public double prevYaw = 0.0;
    public double prevPitch = 0.0;
    public long lastRotationChange = 0L;
    public long lastArmSwing = 0L;
    /** {timestampMs, yaw, pitch} samples for aim analysis */
    public final ArrayDeque<double[]> rotationSamples = new ArrayDeque<>();
    /** |pitch deltas| of recent moves, for the mouse-GCD test */
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> pitchDeltaSamples = new ArrayDeque<>();
    /** recently damaged entities: id -> {timeMs, angleAtHit} */
    public final Map<UUID, double[]> recentTargets = new HashMap<>();
    public int auraSnapBuffer = 0;
    public int auraGcdBuffer = 0;
    public int auraPacingBuffer = 0;
    public int auraSwingBuffer = 0;
    public int auraLockBuffer = 0;
    public int auraMultiBuffer = 0;
    public int auraAngleBuffer = 0;
    public int auraRateBuffer = 0;
    public int auraPerfectBuffer = 0;
    public int cooldownLockBuffer = 0;
    public int critRhythmBuffer = 0;
    public long lastAttackAt = 0L;

    // ---- anti-KB ("Velocity" modules) ----
    public double pendingKbLen = 0;
    public long pendingKbTime = 0L;
    public double kbActual = 0;
    public int antiKbBuffer = 0;

    // ---- misc module coverage ----
    public int nowebBuffer = 0;
    public int elytraSpeedBuffer = 0;
    public int invMoveBuffer = 0;
    public int chestStealBuffer = 0;
    public final ArrayDeque<Long> invClickTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();
    // GUI state tracked via InventoryOpen/CloseEvent (avoids version-fragile InventoryView)
    public boolean guiOpen = false;
    public String guiTypeName = "";

    // ---- silent rotations (oscillating pitch) ----
    public final ArrayDeque<Long> pitchReversals = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public int lastPitchDir = 0;
    public long lastReversalTime = 0L;
    public int silentRotBuffer = 0;

    // ---- xray statistics (per session) ----
    public long minedStone = 0;
    public long minedValuable = 0;
    public long xrayFlagCount = 0;
    public long xrayWindowStart = 0L;

    // ---- gui attacks ----
    public int guiAttackBuffer = 0;

    // ---- packet position jumps (netty thread writes) ----
    public volatile double lastPacketX = Double.NaN;
    public volatile double lastPacketY = Double.NaN;
    public volatile double lastPacketZ = Double.NaN;
    public volatile long lastPosJump = 0L;
    public volatile double lastPosJumpDist = 0;

    // ---- yaw gcd ----
    public final ArrayDeque<Double> yawDeltaSamples = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public int auraGcdYawBuffer = 0;

    // ---- blocks ----
    public final ArrayDeque<Long> breakTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public final ArrayDeque<Long> placeTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public long lastRegenTime = 0L;
    public long consumeStart = 0L;
    public long bowDrawTime = 0L;
    public int speedBuffer = 0;
    public int scaffoldBuffer = 0;
    public int wallClimbBuffer = 0;
    public double wallClimbStartY = 0;

    // ---- nofall ----
    public boolean pendingFallDamage = false;
    public double pendingFallDistance = 0.0;
    public long pendingFallDeadline = 0L;

    // ---- physics simulation (Grim-lite) ----
    public double expectedVy = 0.0;
    public double flyDivergence = 0.0;
    public int stepBuffer = 0;
    public int longJumpBuffer = 0;
    public int waterSpeedBuffer = 0;
    public int elytraRiseSamples = 0;
    public int airStuckTicks = 0;
    public int noSlowBuffer = 0;
    public int groundSpoofStreak = 0;
    public int critsBuffer = 0;
    public long lastFireworkUse = 0L;

    // ---- packet layer (written from the netty thread: volatile / LongAdder) ----
    public volatile long lastFlyingPacket = 0L;
    // default FALSE: until the first flying packet is actually seen we must not
    // assume the client claims ground (would false-flag jumps on join / failed inject)
    public volatile boolean lastPacketGround = false;
    public volatile boolean packetSeen = false;
    public volatile long lastBadPitch = 0L;
    public final java.util.concurrent.atomic.LongAdder packetCounter =
            new java.util.concurrent.atomic.LongAdder();
    public long timerHighRateSince = 0L;

    // ---- lag-compensated reach: position history {timeMs, x, y, z}, 20Hz ----
    public final ArrayDeque<double[]> posHistory = new ArrayDeque<>();

    // ---- violations / AI ----
    public final Map<CheckType, Integer> violations = new EnumMap<>(CheckType.class);
    /** punishment stages already fired: "CHECKTYPE:at" */
    public final java.util.Set<String> firedStages = new java.util.HashSet<>();
    public long lastViolationTime = 0L;
    public double aiProbability = 0.0;
    public long lastAiAlert = 0L;
    public PlayerProfile profile = new PlayerProfile(8);
    public int cleanSamplesDonated = 0;

    public PlayerData(UUID uuid, String name) {
        this.uuid = uuid;
        this.name = name;
    }

    public boolean velocityExempt() {
        return System.currentTimeMillis() < velocityExemptUntil;
    }

    public void exemptVelocity(long ms) {
        velocityExemptUntil = Math.max(velocityExemptUntil, System.currentTimeMillis() + ms);
    }

    public int vl(CheckType type) {
        return violations.getOrDefault(type, 0);
    }

    public void addVl(CheckType type, int amount) {
        violations.merge(type, amount, Integer::sum);
        lastViolationTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public void decayVl(int amount) {
        violations.replaceAll((type, vl) -> Math.max(0, vl - amount));
        violations.values().removeIf(v -> v <= 0);
        // let punishment stages fire again if VL decayed below them
        firedStages.removeIf(key -> {
            int idx = key.indexOf(':');
            if (idx < 0) return true;
            int at = Integer.parseInt(key.substring(idx + 1));
            try {
                CheckType type = CheckType.valueOf(key.substring(0, idx));
                return vl(type) < at;
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                return true;
            }
        });
    }

    public int totalVl() {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int v : violations.values()) sum += v;
        return sum;
    }

    // ---- buffer helpers ----

    public static void push(ArrayDeque<?> deque, Object value, int cap) {
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        ArrayDeque<Object> d = (ArrayDeque<Object>) deque;
        d.addLast(value);
        while (d.size() > cap) d.pollFirst();
    }

    public static void pushTime(ArrayDeque<Long> deque, long time, int cap) {
        deque.addLast(time);
        while (deque.size() > cap) deque.pollFirst();
    }

    public static void prune(ArrayDeque<Long> times, long olderThan) {
        while (!times.isEmpty() && times.peekFirst() < olderThan) times.pollFirst();
    }

    public static double stddev(double[] values) {
        if (values.length < 2) return 0.0;
        double mean = 0.0;
        for (double v : values) mean += v;
        mean /= values.length;
        double acc = 0.0;
        for (double v : values) acc += (v - mean) * (v - mean);
        return Math.sqrt(acc / (values.length - 1));
    }

    public static double avg(double[] values) {
        if (values.length == 0) return 0.0;
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (double v : values) sum += v;
        return sum / values.length;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/listener/ConnectionListener.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.listener;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryCloseEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryOpenEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerGameModeChangeEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRespawnEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerVelocityEvent;

/**
 * Session lifecycle + velocity/teleport exemptions.
 * Any knockback or server-side movement grants a short grace period
 * so movement checks do not false-positive.
 */
public final class ConnectionListener implements Listener {

    private final WepAC plugin;

    public ConnectionListener(WepAC plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        data.name = event.getPlayer().getName();
        data.joinTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        data.lastLocation = event.getPlayer().getLocation().clone();
        data.lastTeleport = System.currentTimeMillis();
        data.exemptVelocity(3000L);
        // hook the packet sniffer into this player's connection
        if (plugin.packets() != null) {
            plugin.packets().inject(event.getPlayer());
        }
        // "police record": announce watched/wanted players to staff
        plugin.records().announceJoin(event.getPlayer());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        if (plugin.packets() != null) {
            plugin.packets().eject(event.getPlayer());
        }
        plugin.data().remove(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        data.lastTeleport = System.currentTimeMillis();
        data.lastLocation = event.getTo() != null ? event.getTo().clone() : null;
        data.airTicks = 0;
        data.hoverTicks = 0;
        data.jesusTicks = 0;
        data.pendingFallDamage = false;
        data.pendingFallDistance = 0;
        data.exemptVelocity(1500L);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        data.lastRespawn = System.currentTimeMillis();
        data.lastLocation = event.getRespawnLocation().clone();
        data.exemptVelocity(3000L);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onGameMode(PlayerGameModeChangeEvent event) {
        plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer()).exemptVelocity(2000L);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVelocity(PlayerVelocityEvent event) {
        plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer()).exemptVelocity(1200L);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player)) return;
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        // knockback from any damage source grants movement grace
        data.exemptVelocity(1500L);

        // capture the server-applied knockback next tick for the anti-KB check:
        // "Velocity" modules (CatLean's flagship) absorb 85-90% of it
        if (event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.FALL) return;
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> {
            if (!player.isOnline() || player.isBlocking()) return;
            if (player.hasPermission("wepac.bypass")) return;
            org.bukkit.util.Vector v = player.getVelocity();
            double h = Math.sqrt(v.getX() * v.getX() + v.getZ() * v.getZ());
            if (h > 0.15) {
                PlayerData d = plugin.data().get(player);
                d.pendingKbLen = h;
                d.pendingKbTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
                d.kbActual = 0;
            }
        });
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteract(org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        org.bukkit.event.block.Action action = event.getAction();
        if (action != org.bukkit.event.block.Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR
                && action != org.bukkit.event.block.Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) {
            return;
        }
        if (event.getItem() != null
                && event.getItem().getType() == org.bukkit.Material.FIREWORK_ROCKET) {
            // legit elytra boost - exempt ElytraFly climb detection briefly
            plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer()).lastFireworkUse = System.currentTimeMillis();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onWorldChange(org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerChangedWorldEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        // mining statistics are per-world; don't carry luck/ore-rates across worlds
        data.minedStone = 0;
        data.minedValuable = 0;
        data.xrayWindowStart = 0L;
        // position history is world-specific: stale coords from the old world
        // would corrupt lag-compensated Reach right after the switch
        data.posHistory.clear();
        // movement state is invalid across a world switch
        data.lastLocation = event.getPlayer().getLocation().clone();
        data.lastGroundLocation = null;
        data.lastTeleport = System.currentTimeMillis();
        data.pendingFallDamage = false;
        data.pendingFallDistance = 0;
        data.hoverTicks = 0;
        data.airTicks = 0;
        data.exemptVelocity(1500L);
    }

    // GUI open/close tracking via events (Inventory#getType is version-stable,
    // unlike InventoryView which became an interface on Paper 1.21+).
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onInventoryOpen(InventoryOpenEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getPlayer() instanceof Player player)) return;
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        data.guiOpen = true;
        try {
            data.guiTypeName = event.getInventory().getType().name();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            data.guiTypeName = "CONTAINER";
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInventoryClose(InventoryCloseEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getPlayer() instanceof Player player)) return;
        plugin.data().get(player).guiOpen = false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/packet/PacketSniffer.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.packet;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.Check;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import io.netty.channel.Channel;
import io.netty.channel.ChannelDuplexHandler;
import io.netty.channel.ChannelHandlerContext;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Packet layer WITHOUT external dependencies: a ChannelDuplexHandler is injected
 * directly into each player's netty pipeline (the same technique protocol
 * libraries use). It observes the client's flying packets and provides the data
 * for the packet-grade checks that event-based anti-cheats cannot do:
 *
 *  - TIMER:       vanilla sends exactly ~20 flying packets/sec; timer hacks 25-40.
 *  - BLINK:       packet stream stops while the player keeps moving.
 *  - GROUND_SPOOF: client claims onGround=true while server-side physics says airborne
 *                 (the root signal of packet Criticals and part of Fly).
 *  - BADPACKETS:  impossible pitch values (|pitch| > 90).
 *
 * Netty-thread safety: the handler only writes volatile fields / a LongAdder on
 * PlayerData; all flagging is evaluated on the main thread (1Hz task here +
 * MovementMonitor).
 */
public final class PacketSniffer {

    private static final String HANDLER_NAME = "wepac_sniffer";

    private final WepAC plugin;
    private final Map<UUID, Channel> channels = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private boolean available = true;

    public PacketSniffer(WepAC plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void start() {
        for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            inject(p);
        }
        // 1Hz evaluation of timer / blink / badpackets
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(plugin, this::evaluate, 20L, 20L);
        plugin.getLogger().info("Packet sniffer active (direct netty hook).");
    }

    public void stop() {
        for (UUID id : channels.keySet()) {
            Player p = Bukkit.getPlayer(id);
            if (p != null) eject(p);
        }
        channels.clear();
    }

    public void inject(Player player) {
        if (!available) return;
        try {
            Object handle = player.getClass().getMethod("getHandle").invoke(player);
            Channel channel = findChannel(handle, 0);
            if (channel == null) {
                warnOnce("Could not locate netty channel for " + player.getName()
                        + "; packet checks inactive for them.");
                return;
            }
            if (channel.pipeline().get(HANDLER_NAME) != null) {
                channels.put(player.getUniqueId(), channel);
                return;
            }
            final UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
            channel.pipeline().addBefore("packet_handler", HANDLER_NAME,
                    new ChannelDuplexHandler() {
                        @Override
                        public void channelRead(ChannelHandlerContext ctx, Object msg) throws Exception {
                            try {
                                onPacket(uuid, msg);
                            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                                // never break the connection
                            }
                            super.channelRead(ctx, msg);
                        }
                    });
            channels.put(player.getUniqueId(), channel);
            // a successful injection re-arms warnings for any future failure
            warned = false;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            // a single player's failure must not disable packet checks for everyone;
            // log once and keep trying for other players / future joins
            warnOnce("Packet injection failed for " + player.getName() + ": " + t);
        }
    }

    private volatile boolean warned = false;

    private void warnOnce(String msg) {
        if (!warned) {
            warned = true;
            plugin.getLogger().warning(msg + " (further injection failures will be silent)");
        }
    }

    public void eject(Player player) {
        Channel channel = channels.remove(player.getUniqueId());
        if (channel == null) return;
        try {
            if (channel.pipeline().get(HANDLER_NAME) != null) {
                channel.pipeline().remove(HANDLER_NAME);
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Netty thread: record only, never flag
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private void onPacket(UUID uuid, Object msg) {
        // IMPORTANT: movement packets are NESTED classes
        // (ServerboundMovePlayerPacket$Pos/$Rot/$PosRot/$Status on Mojang mappings,
        // PacketPlayInFlying$PacketPlayInPosition/... on Spigot mappings).
        // Class#getSimpleName() of a nested class drops the outer name ("Pos",
        // "PacketPlayInPosition"), so we MUST match on the full binary name.
        String cls = msg.getClass().getName();
        boolean flying = cls.contains("PacketPlayInFlying")
                || cls.contains("ServerboundMovePlayerPacket");
        if (!flying) return;

        PlayerData data = plugin.data().getIfLoaded(uuid);
        if (data == null) return;

        data.packetCounter.increment();
        data.lastFlyingPacket = System.currentTimeMillis();

        Boolean ground = readBooleanField(msg, "onGround");
        if (ground != null) {
            data.lastPacketGround = ground;
            data.packetSeen = true;
        }
        Float pitch = readFloatField(msg, "xRot", "pitch");
        if (pitch != null && Math.abs(pitch) > 90.5f) {
            data.lastBadPitch = System.currentTimeMillis();
        }

        // position jump: a single move packet claiming a teleport-sized delta
        Double x = readDoubleField(msg, "x");
        Double y = readDoubleField(msg, "y");
        Double z = readDoubleField(msg, "z");
        if (x != null && y != null && z != null) {
            double px = data.lastPacketX, py = data.lastPacketY, pz = data.lastPacketZ;
            data.lastPacketX = x;
            data.lastPacketY = y;
            data.lastPacketZ = z;
            if (!Double.isNaN(px)) {
                double dx = x - px, dy = y - py, dz = z - pz;
                double dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
                long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
                boolean grace = now - data.lastTeleport < 5000L
                        || now - data.lastRespawn < 5000L
                        || now - data.joinTime < 5000L;
                if (dist > 16.0 && !grace) {
                    data.lastPosJumpDist = dist;
                    data.lastPosJump = now;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static final Map<Class<?>, Field> FIELD_CACHE = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private static Boolean readBooleanField(Object packet, String... names) {
        Field f = resolveField(packet.getClass(), Boolean.TYPE, names);
        if (f == null) return null;
        try {
            return f.getBoolean(packet);
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static Float readFloatField(Object packet, String... names) {
        Field f = resolveField(packet.getClass(), Float.TYPE, names);
        if (f == null) return null;
        try {
            return f.getFloat(packet);
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static Double readDoubleField(Object packet, String... names) {
        Field f = resolveField(packet.getClass(), Double.TYPE, names);
        if (f == null) return null;
        try {
            return f.getDouble(packet);
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /** Field by name first (walking superclasses); fallback: unique primitive of that type. */
    private static Field resolveField(Class<?> clazz, Class<?> type, String... names) {
        for (String name : names) {
            Class<?> c = clazz;
            while (c != null && c != Object.class) {
                try {
                    Field f = c.getDeclaredField(name);
                    if (f.getType() == type) {
                        f.setAccessible(true);
                        return f;
                    }
                } catch (NoSuchFieldException ignored) {
                }
                c = c.getSuperclass();
            }
        }
        Field cached = FIELD_CACHE.get(clazz);
        if (cached != null && cached.getType() == type) return cached;
        Field unique = null;
        Class<?> c = clazz;
        while (c != null && c != Object.class) {
            for (Field f : c.getDeclaredFields()) {
                if (f.getType() == type && !java.lang.reflect.Modifier.isStatic(f.getModifiers())) {
                    if (unique != null) return null; // ambiguous - refuse to guess
                    unique = f;
                }
            }
            c = c.getSuperclass();
        }
        if (unique != null) {
            unique.setAccessible(true);
            FIELD_CACHE.put(clazz, unique);
            return unique;
        }
        return null;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Main thread: evaluation
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private long lastEvaluate = System.currentTimeMillis();

    private void evaluate() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        // Normalise by the REAL elapsed wall time: under server lag this task can
        // run later than 1s apart, which would otherwise inflate packets/sec and
        // false-flag Timer. Guard against absurd gaps (freeze) too.
        long elapsed = now - lastEvaluate;
        lastEvaluate = now;
        if (elapsed < 200L || elapsed > 5000L) {
            // too short to measure, or the server froze - drop this window's counts
            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                PlayerData d = plugin.data().getIfLoaded(p.getUniqueId());
                if (d != null) d.packetCounter.sumThenReset();
            }
            return;
        }
        int maxRate = plugin.config().timerMaxRate;
        long sustainMs = plugin.config().timerSustainMs;
        long blinkTimeout = plugin.config().blinkTimeoutMs;
        int lagPing = plugin.config().lagPingThreshold;

        for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
          try {
            PlayerData data = plugin.data().getIfLoaded(player.getUniqueId());
            if (data == null) continue;
            if (player.hasPermission("wepac.bypass")) continue;

            long count = data.packetCounter.sumThenReset();
            double rate = count * 1000.0 / elapsed; // packets per second, lag-normalised

            // TIMER (skip very high ping - their packets arrive in bursts)
            Check timer = plugin.check(CheckType.TIMER);
            if (timer != null && timer.enabled()
                    && now - data.joinTime > 5000L
                    && now - data.lastTeleport > 2000L
                    && player.getPing() <= lagPing) {
                if (rate > maxRate) {
                    if (data.timerHighRateSince == 0L) {
                        data.timerHighRateSince = now;
                    } else if (now - data.timerHighRateSince >= sustainMs) {
                        timer.flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                                "%.1f move-packets/sec (vanilla: 20)", rate), 4);
                        data.timerHighRateSince = 0L;
                    }
                } else {
                    data.timerHighRateSince = 0L;
                }
            }

            // BLINK (riders send vehicle-move packets, not player position packets;
            // dead players send none - never false-flag either)
            boolean sendsPositionPackets = !player.isDead() && !player.isInsideVehicle();
            Check blink = plugin.check(CheckType.BLINK);
            if (blink != null && blink.enabled() && sendsPositionPackets
                    && data.lastFlyingPacket != 0L
                    && now - data.lastFlyingPacket > blinkTimeout
                    && now - data.joinTime > 5000L) {
                blink.flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "no move packets for %dms", now - data.lastFlyingPacket), 3);
                data.lastFlyingPacket = now; // avoid alert spam
            }

            // POSITION JUMP (single-packet teleport hacks)
            if (blink != null && blink.enabled() && data.lastPosJump != 0L
                    && now - data.lastPosJump < 1500L) {
                blink.flag(player, data, String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                        "position jumped %.1f blocks in a single packet", data.lastPosJumpDist), 4);
                data.lastPosJump = 0L;
            }

            // BADPACKETS
            Check bad = plugin.check(CheckType.BADPACKETS);
            if (bad != null && bad.enabled() && data.lastBadPitch != 0L) {
                bad.flag(player, data, "impossible pitch value in rotation packet", 5);
                data.lastBadPitch = 0L;
            }
          } catch (Throwable t) {
              plugin.debug("PacketSniffer evaluate error for " + player.getName() + ": " + t);
          }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Reflection helper: find the netty Channel below the NMS entity handle
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static Channel findChannel(Object obj, int depth) {
        if (obj == null || depth > 4) return null;
        if (obj instanceof Channel) return (Channel) obj;
        Class<?> clazz = obj.getClass();
        while (clazz != null && clazz != Object.class) {
            for (Field f : clazz.getDeclaredFields()) {
                if (java.lang.reflect.Modifier.isStatic(f.getModifiers())) continue;
                Class<?> ft = f.getType();
                if (Channel.class.isAssignableFrom(ft)) {
                    try {
                        f.setAccessible(true);
                        Object v = f.get(obj);
                        if (v instanceof Channel) return (Channel) v;
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    }
                }
            }
            clazz = clazz.getSuperclass();
        }
        // second pass: descend into non-primitive complex fields (connection objects)
        clazz = obj.getClass();
        while (clazz != null && clazz != Object.class) {
            for (Field f : clazz.getDeclaredFields()) {
                if (java.lang.reflect.Modifier.isStatic(f.getModifiers())) continue;
                Class<?> ft = f.getType();
                if (ft.isPrimitive() || ft.getName().startsWith("java.")
                        || ft.getName().startsWith("io.netty.") || ft.isArray()
                        || ft.getName().startsWith("it.unimi.")
                        || ft.getName().startsWith("com.mojang.")) {
                    continue;
                }
                try {
                    f.setAccessible(true);
                    Object v = f.get(obj);
                    Channel c = findChannel(v, depth + 1);
                    if (c != null) return c;
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                }
            }
            clazz = clazz.getSuperclass();
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/punish/PunishmentManager.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.punish;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Executes configured punishment commands - but only when the anti-cheat is
 * CONVINCED (see confirm section): kick/ban stages are deferred and retried
 * on every further VL until confirmation passes. Confirmed strikes feed the
 * AI training data and the police-record system.
 */
public final class PunishmentManager {

    private final WepAC plugin;
    private final Map<String, Long> deferNotifyCooldown = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public PunishmentManager(WepAC plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    /**
     * @return true when the stage was executed (or was alert-only) and must
     *         not be retried; false when it was deferred for lack of confirmation.
     */
    public boolean tryExecute(Player player, CheckType type, int vl, String command) {
        if (command == null || command.isEmpty()) return true; // alert-only stage

        String cmd = command
                .replace("%player%", player.getName())
                .replace("%check%", type.displayName())
                .replace("%vl%", String.valueOf(vl));

        boolean harsh = isHarsh(cmd);
        if (harsh && plugin.config().confirmEnabled && !confirmed(player, type)) {
            notifyDeferred(player, type, vl);
            return false;
        }

        // Confirmed punishment -> training data for the AI (before the player leaves)
        plugin.ai().labelFromPunishment(player);

        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () ->
                Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), cmd));

        plugin.getLogger().info("Punished " + player.getName() + " [" + type.displayName()
                + " VL " + vl + "]: " + cmd);

        if (harsh) {
            plugin.records().addStrike(player, type, isBan(cmd));
        }
        return true;
    }

    /** Legacy direct execution (used by AI-only punishments). */
    public void execute(Player player, CheckType type, int vl, String command) {
        tryExecute(player, type, vl, command);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private boolean confirmed(Player player, CheckType type) {
        PlayerData d = plugin.data().get(player);

        long distinct = d.violations.values().stream().filter(v -> v > 0).count();
        if (distinct >= plugin.config().confirmMinDistinctChecks) return true;

        int threshold = plugin.config().threshold(type);
        double recordMul = plugin.records().thresholdMultiplier(player.getUniqueId());
        if (d.vl(type) >= threshold * recordMul * plugin.config().confirmSingleVlMultiplier) return true;

        return d.aiProbability >= plugin.config().confirmAiProbability;
    }

    /** First word of a command, with leading '/' and any "namespace:" stripped. */
    private static String commandWord(String cmd) {
        String s = cmd.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (s.startsWith("/")) s = s.substring(1);
        int space = s.indexOf(' ');
        String head = space < 0 ? s : s.substring(0, space);
        int colon = head.indexOf(':');
        if (colon >= 0 && colon < head.length() - 1) {
            head = head.substring(colon + 1); // essentials:kick -> kick
        }
        return head;
    }

    private static boolean isHarsh(String cmd) {
        String w = commandWord(cmd);
        return w.equals("kick") || w.equals("ban") || w.equals("tempban")
                || w.equals("ban-ip") || w.equals("banip") || w.equals("tkb");
    }

    private static boolean isBan(String cmd) {
        String w = commandWord(cmd);
        return w.equals("ban") || w.equals("tempban")
                || w.equals("ban-ip") || w.equals("banip");
    }

    private void notifyDeferred(Player player, CheckType type, int vl) {
        String key = player.getUniqueId() + ":" + type.name();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        // bound the map: drop entries whose cooldown has long expired
        if (deferNotifyCooldown.size() > 512) {
            deferNotifyCooldown.values().removeIf(t -> now - t > 60_000L);
        }
        Long last = deferNotifyCooldown.get(key);
        if (last != null && now - last < 30_000L) return;
        deferNotifyCooldown.put(key, now);

        plugin.alerts().broadcastRaw(String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                "&e[вердикт не окончательный]&r &7%s &7(&c%s&7, VL %d): наказание отложено. "
                        + "Нужно: &f%d+ &7разных проверок, &fVL %.0f+ &7по этой, или уверенность ИИ &f%.0f%%&7.",
                player.getName(), type.displayName(), vl,
                plugin.config().confirmMinDistinctChecks,
                plugin.config().threshold(type)
                        * plugin.records().thresholdMultiplier(player.getUniqueId())
                        * plugin.config().confirmSingleVlMultiplier,
                plugin.config().confirmAiProbability * 100));
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/record/RecordManager.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.record;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * The "police record" system.
 *
 *  CLEAN  -> first confirmed kick puts the player ON RECORD (WATCH):
 *            thresholds shrink, every flag is shown to staff, join is announced.
 *  WATCH  -> another strike triggers a FULL BEHAVIOUR REVIEW (dossier score
 *            from all active VLs, distinct checks, AI probability, history).
 *            score >= review-ban-score  -> WANTED + auto-ban + rage alert;
 *            below                      -> the anti-cheat complains VERY loudly.
 *  WANTED -> any further strike keeps screaming for a ban.
 *
 * Records persist in records.yml and survive restarts.
 */
public final class RecordManager {

    public enum Status { CLEAN, WATCH, WANTED }

    public static final class Record {
        public Status status = Status.CLEAN;
        public int kicks = 0;
        public int bans = 0;
        public long since = 0L;
        public final List<String> notes = new ArrayList<>();
    }

    private static final SimpleDateFormat DATE = new SimpleDateFormat("dd.MM HH:mm");

    private final WepAC plugin;
    private final Map<UUID, Record> records = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public RecordManager(WepAC plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Persistence
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private File file() {
        return new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), plugin.config().recordFile);
    }

    public void load() {
        File f = file();
        if (!f.exists()) return;
        YamlConfiguration y = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(f);
        for (String key : y.getKeys(false)) {
            UUID id;
            try {
                id = UUID.fromString(key);
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                continue;
            }
            Record r = new Record();
            r.status = parseStatus(y.getString(key + ".status", "CLEAN"));
            r.kicks = y.getInt(key + ".kicks", 0);
            r.bans = y.getInt(key + ".bans", 0);
            r.since = y.getLong(key + ".since", 0L);
            r.notes.addAll(y.getStringList(key + ".notes"));
            records.put(id, r);
        }
        plugin.getLogger().info("Loaded " + records.size() + " criminal records.");
    }

    public void save() {
        YamlConfiguration y = new YamlConfiguration();
        for (Map.Entry<UUID, Record> e : records.entrySet()) {
            String key = e.getKey().toString();
            Record r = e.getValue();
            if (r.status == Status.CLEAN && r.kicks == 0 && r.bans == 0 && r.notes.isEmpty()) {
                continue; // don't store clean players
            }
            y.set(key + ".status", r.status.name());
            y.set(key + ".kicks", r.kicks);
            y.set(key + ".bans", r.bans);
            y.set(key + ".since", r.since);
            y.set(key + ".notes", r.notes);
        }
        try {
            File f = file();
            File parent = f.getParentFile();
            if (parent != null && !parent.exists()) parent.mkdirs();
            y.save(f);
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            plugin.getLogger().severe("Failed to save records: " + ex.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static Status parseStatus(String s) {
        try {
            return Status.valueOf(s.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return Status.CLEAN;
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Access
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public Record get(UUID id) {
        return records.computeIfAbsent(id, k -> new Record());
    }

    public Status status(UUID id) {
        return get(id).status;
    }

    /** Threshold multiplier for watched/wanted players (stricter checks). */
    public double thresholdMultiplier(UUID id) {
        if (!plugin.config().recordEnabled) return 1.0;
        return switch (status(id)) {
            case WATCH -> plugin.config().watchMultiplier;
            case WANTED -> plugin.config().wantedMultiplier;
            default -> 1.0;
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Strikes
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Called by PunishmentManager after a confirmed kick/ban executed. */
    public void addStrike(Player player, CheckType type, boolean isBan) {
        if (!plugin.config().recordEnabled) return;
        // anti-poisoning: wipe any "legit PvP" samples this player ever donated
        plugin.ai().purgeSamplesFrom(player.getUniqueId());
        Record r = get(player.getUniqueId());
        String note = DATE.format(new Date()) + " " + type.displayName() + (isBan ? " BAN" : " KICK");
        if (isBan) r.bans++;
        else r.kicks++;
        r.notes.add(note);
        while (r.notes.size() > 50) r.notes.remove(0);

        if (isBan) {
            r.status = Status.WANTED;
            r.since = System.currentTimeMillis();
            plugin.alerts().broadcastRaw("&4&l[РОЗЫСК]&r &c" + player.getName()
                    + " &7забанен (&c" + type.displayName() + "&7) — внесён в базу как рецидивист.");
        } else if (r.status == Status.CLEAN) {
            r.status = Status.WATCH;
            r.since = System.currentTimeMillis();
            plugin.alerts().broadcastRaw("&6&l[НА УЧЁТ]&r &7" + player.getName()
                    + " &7кикнут впервые (&c" + type.displayName()
                    + "&7). Поставлен на учёт: пристальное наблюдение, пороги ужесточены.");
        } else if (r.status == Status.WATCH) {
            review(player, r, type);
        } else {
            plugin.alerts().rage(player, "рецидив в статусе В РОЗЫСКЕ (" + type.displayName() + ")");
        }
        save();
    }

    /** Full behaviour review: builds the dossier score and decides ban vs complain. */
    private void review(Player player, Record r, CheckType type) {
        double score = reviewScore(player);
        PlayerData d = plugin.data().get(player);
        long distinct = d.violations.values().stream().filter(v -> v > 0).count();

        plugin.alerts().broadcastRaw(String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                "&4&l[ПРОВЕРКА ПОВЕДЕНИЯ]&r &7%s &7пойман повторно (&c%s&7). Досье: "
                        + "&fscore %.0f&7, активных проверок &f%d&7, суммарный VL &f%d&7, ИИ &f%.0f%%&7, страйков &f%d",
                player.getName(), type.displayName(), score, distinct,
                d.totalVl(), d.aiProbability * 100, r.kicks + r.bans));

        r.notes.add(DATE.format(new Date()) + String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                " REVIEW score=%.0f (%s)", score, type.displayName()));

        if (score >= plugin.config().reviewBanScore) {
            r.status = Status.WANTED;
            String cmd = plugin.config().recordBanCommand
                    .replace("%player%", player.getName())
                    .replace("%check%", type.displayName())
                    .replace("%score%", String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.0f", score));
            plugin.ai().labelFromPunishment(player);
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () ->
                    Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), cmd));
            plugin.getLogger().info("RECORD-BAN " + player.getName() + " score=" + score);
            plugin.alerts().rage(player, "повторные читы после учёта, score " + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.0f", score));
        } else {
            // not conclusive enough for an auto-ban -> complain VERY loudly
            plugin.alerts().rage(player, "подозрителен, но для автобана не хватило score ("
                    + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.0f", score) + "/" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.0f", plugin.config().reviewBanScore) + ")");
        }
    }

    /** Dossier score: everything we know about this player, in one number. */
    public double reviewScore(Player player) {
        PlayerData d = plugin.data().get(player);
        long distinct = d.violations.values().stream().filter(v -> v > 0).count();
        Record r = get(player.getUniqueId());
        return distinct * 20.0
                + Math.min(d.totalVl(), 100) * 0.5
                + d.aiProbability * 40.0
                + (r.kicks + r.bans) * 15.0;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Manual control
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public void setStatus(Player player, Status status, String byWhom) {
        Record r = get(player.getUniqueId());
        r.status = status;
        if (status != Status.CLEAN && r.since == 0L) r.since = System.currentTimeMillis();
        r.notes.add(DATE.format(new Date()) + " status -> " + status + " by " + byWhom);
        save();
        plugin.alerts().broadcastRaw("&7Статус &f" + player.getName() + "&7: &6"
                + statusLabel(status) + " &8(" + byWhom + ")");
    }

    public void clear(UUID id) {
        records.remove(id);
        save();
    }

    public static String statusLabel(Status s) {
        return switch (s) {
            case CLEAN -> "&aчист";
            case WATCH -> "&6НА УЧЁТЕ";
            case WANTED -> "&c&lВ РОЗЫСКЕ";
        };
    }

    /** Join announcement for staff. */
    public void announceJoin(Player player) {
        if (!plugin.config().recordEnabled) return;
        Status s = status(player.getUniqueId());
        if (s == Status.WATCH) {
            plugin.alerts().broadcastRaw("&6&l[УЧЁТ]&r &7" + player.getName()
                    + " &7вошёл на сервер. Он &6на учёте&7 — пристальное наблюдение активно.");
        } else if (s == Status.WANTED) {
            plugin.alerts().broadcastRaw("&4&l[РОЗЫСК]&r &c" + player.getName()
                    + " &7вошёл на сервер. Он &c&lВ РОЗЫСКЕ&7 — при любом нарушении банить немедленно.");
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/util/Compat.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.util;

import org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Version-compatibility helpers.
 *
 * Bukkit 1.20.5+ moved Enchantment / PotionEffectType to registries and Paper
 * turned InventoryView into an interface. A plugin compiled against one version
 * can hit NoSuchFieldError / IncompatibleClassChangeError at runtime on another.
 * These helpers resolve enchantments/effects by their stable string KEY (iterating
 * what the item/player actually has) and never touch version-specific static
 * fields, and every lookup is wrapped so a failure degrades gracefully instead
 * of crashing the plugin.
 */
public final class Compat {

    private Compat() {
    }

    /** Level of the first matching enchantment on the item (0 if none / on error). */
    public static int enchantLevel(ItemStack item, String... keyNames) {
        if (item == null || !item.hasItemMeta()) return 0;
        try {
            for (Map.Entry<Enchantment, Integer> e : item.getEnchantments().entrySet()) {
                Enchantment en = e.getKey();
                if (en == null) continue;
                String k = enchantKey(en);
                for (String want : keyNames) {
                    if (want.equalsIgnoreCase(k)) {
                        return e.getValue() == null ? 0 : e.getValue();
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return 0;
    }

    public static boolean hasEnchant(ItemStack item, String... keyNames) {
        return enchantLevel(item, keyNames) > 0;
    }

    private static String enchantKey(Enchantment en) {
        try {
            return en.getKey().getKey();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            try {
                return en.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            } catch (Throwable t2) {
                return "";
            }
        }
    }

    /** True if the player has an active effect matching any of the given keys. */
    public static boolean hasEffect(Player player, String... keyNames) {
        return effectAmplifier(player, keyNames) >= 0;
    }

    /** Amplifier+1 (level) of the first matching active effect, or -1 if absent. */
    public static int effectAmplifier(Player player, String... keyNames) {
        if (player == null) return -1;
        try {
            for (PotionEffect eff : player.getActivePotionEffects()) {
                if (eff == null) continue;
                String k = effectKey(eff.getType());
                for (String want : keyNames) {
                    if (want.equalsIgnoreCase(k)) return eff.getAmplifier() + 1;
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return -1;
    }

    private static String effectKey(PotionEffectType t) {
        if (t == null) return "";
        try {
            return t.getKey().getKey();
        } catch (Throwable e) {
            try {
                return t.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            } catch (Throwable e2) {
                return "";
            }
        }
    }

    // Common key aliases across versions (registry renames in 1.20.5+)
    public static final String[] JUMP = {"jump", "jump_boost"};
    public static final String[] HASTE = {"haste", "fast_digging"};
    public static final String[] SPEED = {"speed"};
    public static final String[] LEVITATION = {"levitation"};
    public static final String[] SLOW_FALLING = {"slow_falling"};
    public static final String[] DOLPHINS = {"dolphins_grace"};
    public static final String[] EFFICIENCY = {"efficiency", "dig_speed"};
    public static final String[] DEPTH_STRIDER = {"depth_strider"};
    public static final String[] SOUL_SPEED = {"soul_speed"};
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/util/GroundUtil.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.util;

import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

import java.util.Set;

public final class GroundUtil {

    private static final Set<Material> CLIMBABLE = Set.of(
            Material.LADDER, Material.VINE, Material.SCAFFOLDING,
            Material.WEEPING_VINES, Material.WEEPING_VINES_PLANT,
            Material.TWISTING_VINES, Material.TWISTING_VINES_PLANT);

    private GroundUtil() {
    }

    /**
     * Server-authoritative ground check based on block bounding boxes.
     * Immune to client "on ground" packet spoofing.
     */
    public static boolean isOnGround(Player player) {
        BoundingBox box = player.getBoundingBox();
        double feet = box.getMinY();
        int y = (int) Math.floor(feet - 0.08);
        int minX = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinX());
        int maxX = (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxX());
        int minZ = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinZ());
        int maxZ = (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxZ());

        for (int x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {
            for (int z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {
                Block block = player.getWorld().getBlockAt(x, y, z);
                // A block supports the player if it has a collision box (not passable).
                // This covers snow layers, carpets, slabs, stairs, scaffolding - not
                // just Material#isSolid() (snow layer / carpet report isSolid()=false,
                // which previously made players standing on them count as airborne).
                if (block.isPassable()) continue;
                BoundingBox bb = block.getBoundingBox();
                // feet rest on (or slightly sink into) the block top
                if (feet <= bb.getMaxY() + 0.08 && feet >= bb.getMaxY() - 0.35) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public static boolean onClimbable(Player player) {
        Block atFeet = player.getLocation().getBlock();
        if (CLIMBABLE.contains(atFeet.getType())) return true;
        Block atEyes = player.getEyeLocation().getBlock();
        return CLIMBABLE.contains(atEyes.getType());
    }

    public static boolean inLiquid(Player player) {
        Block feet = player.getLocation().getBlock();
        if (feet.isLiquid() || feet.getType() == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN) return true;
        Block below = feet.getRelative(0, -1, 0);
        return below.isLiquid() || below.getType() == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN;
    }

    /** True when the player stands on a liquid surface without sinking (Jesus/lava-walk). */
    public static boolean onLiquidSurface(Player player) {
        BoundingBox box = player.getBoundingBox();
        Block feetBlock = player.getLocation().getBlock();
        Block below = feetBlock.getRelative(0, -1, 0);
        Material belowType = below.getType();
        if (belowType != Material.WATER && belowType != Material.LAVA) return false;
        if (feetBlock.getType() == belowType && player.isSwimming()) return false;
        if (belowType == Material.WATER
                && below.getRelative(0, 1, 0).getType() == Material.LILY_PAD) return false;
        // feet must sit right at the surface plane of the liquid block below
        double surface = below.getY() + 1.0;
        return Math.abs(box.getMinY() - surface) < 0.25;
    }

    public static boolean onSlimeOrIce(Player player) {
        Block below = player.getLocation().getBlock().getRelative(0, -1, 0);
        Material m = below.getType();
        return m == Material.SLIME_BLOCK || m.name().contains("ICE");
    }

    public static boolean softLanding(Player player) {
        Block below = player.getLocation().getBlock().getRelative(0, -1, 0);
        Material m = below.getType();
        return m == Material.WATER || m == Material.SLIME_BLOCK
                || m == Material.POWDER_SNOW || m == Material.HAY_BLOCK
                || m.name().endsWith("_BED") || m.name().endsWith("WOOL");
    }

    /**
     * Returns the solid wall block the player is currently pressed against
     * (within 0.12 blocks of the body), or null if there is no wall.
     * Used by the WallClimb/Spider detection: vanilla players cannot move UP
     * along a wall without ladders/vines/scaffolding.
     */
    public static Block adjacentWall(Player player) {
        BoundingBox box = player.getBoundingBox();
        double expand = 0.12;
        int minY = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinY());
        int maxY = (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxY() - 0.01);
        int minX = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinX() - expand);
        int maxX = (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxX() + expand);
        int minZ = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinZ() - expand);
        int maxZ = (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxZ() + expand);

        for (int x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {
            for (int y = minY; y <= maxY; y++) {
                for (int z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {
                    Block block = player.getWorld().getBlockAt(x, y, z);
                    Material m = block.getType();
                    if (!m.isSolid() || CLIMBABLE.contains(m)) continue;
                    BoundingBox bb = block.getBoundingBox();
                    // must touch the player horizontally and overlap vertically
                    boolean touchesX = bb.getMaxX() >= box.getMinX() - expand
                            && bb.getMinX() <= box.getMaxX() + expand
                            && (bb.getMinX() >= box.getMaxX() - expand || bb.getMaxX() <= box.getMinX() + expand);
                    boolean touchesZ = bb.getMaxZ() >= box.getMinZ() - expand
                            && bb.getMinZ() <= box.getMaxZ() + expand
                            && (bb.getMinZ() >= box.getMaxZ() - expand || bb.getMaxZ() <= box.getMinZ() + expand);
                    boolean overlapsY = bb.getMaxY() > box.getMinY() + 0.2
                            && bb.getMinY() < box.getMaxY();
                    if ((touchesX || touchesZ) && overlapsY) {
                        return block;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/weposs/wepac/violation/ViolationManager.java`:

```java
package dev.weposs.wepac.violation;

import dev.weposs.wepac.WepAC;
import dev.weposs.wepac.check.CheckType;
import dev.weposs.wepac.config.ConfigManager;
import dev.weposs.wepac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

/** Routes violations: VL bookkeeping, alerts, punishment stages with retries. */
public final class ViolationManager {

    private final WepAC plugin;

    public ViolationManager(WepAC plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void handle(Player player, PlayerData data, CheckType type, String info,
                       int severity, int threshold) {
        data.addVl(type, severity);
        int vl = data.vl(type);

        plugin.alerts().send(player, type, vl, info);

        if (!plugin.config().punishmentsEnabled()) return;
        if (vl < threshold) return;

        // highest stage whose 'at' is reached
        ConfigManager.PunishStage applicable = null;
        for (ConfigManager.PunishStage stage : plugin.config().stages(type)) {
            if (vl >= stage.at) applicable = stage;
        }
        if (applicable == null) return;

        String stageKey = type.name() + ":" + applicable.at;
        if (data.firedStages.contains(stageKey)) return;

        // PunishmentManager defers kick/ban until the verdict is confirmed;
        // deferred stages are retried on every further VL.
        if (plugin.punishments().tryExecute(player, type, vl, applicable.command)) {
            data.firedStages.add(stageKey);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/resources/config.yml`:

```yml
# ============================================================
#  WepAC - self-learning AI anti-cheat
#  Docs: README.md
# ============================================================

prefix: "&8[&cWepAC&8]&r "

# Staff alerts
alerts:
  # Message sent to staff with wepac.alerts when a check triggers
  format: "{prefix}&7{player} &8| &c{check} &8| &7VL {vl} &8| &7ping {ping}ms &8| &7AI {ai} &8| &f{info}"
  # Also broadcast to console
  console: true

# Violation (VL) decay: removes {amount} VL every {interval} seconds of clean play
decay:
  interval: 30
  amount: 1

# ------------------------------------------------------------
#  FALSE-POSITIVE GUARDS (protect honest players)
# ------------------------------------------------------------
guards:
  # Players with ping above the threshold need twice as many confirmations
  # for movement checks (their telemetry is noisier)
  lag-guard: true
  lag-ping-threshold: 450
  # Teleport confirmed cheaters back to their last legal ground position
  # (Fly / WallClimb / AirJump / Phase). Honest players are never affected:
  # rollback only happens after a check has confirmed via its buffer.
  rollback: true

# Punishments. Commands run when a check reaches the VL 'at' value.
# Placeholders: %player% %check% %vl%
# The FIRST matching stage for a check fires; set command to "" to only alert.
punishments:
  enabled: true
  default-stages:
    - at: 10
      command: ""            # alert only
    - at: 25
      command: "kick %player% &c[WepAC] Unfair advantage detected (%check%)"
    - at: 50
      command: "tempban %player% 1d &c[WepAC] %check%"
  # Per-check overrides. BoatFly is deterministic physics (a boat cannot hover),
  # so it punishes instantly on the first confirmed flag (threshold 3 + severity 5
  # per flag passes the confirm gate at once).
  overrides:
    BOATFLY:
      - at: 3
        command: "tempban %player% 1d &c[WepAC] BoatFly — лодка не умеет летать"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
#  KICK/BAN CONFIRMATION - the anti-cheat only kicks when CONVINCED.
#  A kick/ban stage executes when ANY of these holds:
#   - at least {min-distinct-checks} different checks have active VL,
#   - the triggering check reached threshold * {single-check-vl-multiplier},
#   - the AI probability is at least {ai-probability}.
#  Otherwise the stage is DEFERRED and retried on every further VL.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
confirm:
  enabled: true
  min-distinct-checks: 2
  single-check-vl-multiplier: 1.5
  ai-probability: 0.97

# ------------------------------------------------------------
#  POLICE RECORD ("учёт")
#  First confirmed kick  -> player goes ON RECORD (WATCH): stricter
#    thresholds, join announcements, every flag shown to staff.
#  Another strike while ON RECORD -> full behaviour review (dossier score).
#    score >= review-ban-score -> WANTED + auto-ban + rage alert.
#    below                     -> the anti-cheat complains VERY loudly.
#  Dossier score = distinctChecks*20 + min(totalVL,100)*0.5 + AI*40 + strikes*15
# ------------------------------------------------------------
record:
  enabled: true
  file: records.yml
  watch-threshold-multiplier: 0.6
  wanted-threshold-multiplier: 0.4
  review-ban-score: 100
  ban-command: "tempban %player% 7d &c[WepAC] Рецидив после учёта (%check%, score %score%)"
  # What the anti-cheat screams into the staff console when the verdict is clear.
  rage-message: "&4&l%player%&c&l, ЧИТАК ЕБУЧИЙ СУКА БАНЬТЕ БЛЯТЬ БЫСТРЕЕ НАХУЙ"
  rage-repeat: 3

# ------------------------------------------------------------
#  CLIENT DETECTION
#  On join every client announces itself via the "minecraft:brand" payload.
#   - brand matches cheat-brands      -> 100% certainty, BAN + rage alert
#   - brand suspicious/unknown        -> NOT banned (could be a rare vanilla
#                                        launcher), put ON RECORD and watched
#   - brand matches known-brands      -> reported to staff ("plays from ...")
# ------------------------------------------------------------
client-detect:
  enabled: true
  notify-staff: true
  ban-command: "ban %player% &c[WepAC] Читерский клиент: %client%"
  # Definitive cheat clients (substring match on the lowercased brand)
  cheat-brands:
    # --- verified from client sources/JARs (fabric.mod.json ids, 10-agent sweep) ---
    - catlean
    - vesence
    - recode
    - essence
    - arbuz
    - cometa
    - crystall
    - euphoria
    - evaware
    - skycore
    - wexside
    - xrose
    - zov
    - velka
    - obsidian
    - punch
    - phobia
    - wild
    - winlend
    - wonderful
    - copyright
    - systemdlc
    - aimware
    - exploit
    - britva
    - celestial
    - collapse
    - sadnes
    - brokke
    - minced
    - ryzen
    - toshiba
    - dimasik
    - expa
    - delux
    - vzmax
    - govno
    # --- from the loader/launcher collection ---
    - rockstar
    - nursultan
    - ghost
    - onyx
    - aether
    - xeno
    - delta
    - expensive
    - evolate
    - everlast
    - eternity
    - energy
    - nuclear
    - nexis
    - valhalla
    - wildclient
    - monoton
```
