```yml
  # Suspicious but unproven -> watch only, never ban
  watch-brands:
    - salwyrr
    - modded
  # NOTE: intentionally NOT banned by substring: bare "light" (collides with
  # "flight"), bare "quick"/"beta" (too generic). Add manually if you see them.
  # Known legit clients: substring -> display name for staff messages
  known-brands:
    vanilla: Vanilla
    fabric: Fabric
    forge: Forge
    quilt: Quilt
    optifine: OptiFine
    lunar: Lunar Client
    badlion: Badlion Client
    tlauncher: TLauncher
    hmcl: HMCL
    multimc: MultiMC
    prism: Prism Launcher
    gdlauncher: GDLauncher
    curseforge: CurseForge
  # Completely unknown brand -> watch (ban is NEVER issued without certainty)
  watch-unknown: true

# ------------------------------------------------------------
#  PACKET LAYER (direct netty hook, no external dependencies)
#  Enables Timer / Blink / GroundSpoof / Criticals / BadPackets -
#  checks that are impossible on Bukkit events alone.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
packet-sniffer:
  enabled: true
  # vanilla sends exactly ~20 move packets per second; 21+ sustained = timer
  # (catches even subtle 1.05-1.1x timers like CatLean's 1.088)
  timer-max-rate: 21
  # how long the rate must stay above max before a Timer flag
  timer-sustain-ms: 2500
  # no move packets for this long while online = Blink
  blink-timeout-ms: 2000

# ------------------------------------------------------------
#  SELF-LEARNING AI
# ------------------------------------------------------------
ai:
  enabled: true
  # Neural network training
  learning-rate: 0.02
  batch-size: 32
  # Seconds between online training rounds
  train-interval: 60
  # Seconds between AI scoring passes over online players
  score-interval: 3
  # Save model to disk every N minutes (and on shutdown)
  save-interval-minutes: 5
  model-file: ai/model.bin

  # Auto-labeling of training data:
  auto-label:
    # Player punished (kick/ban) by a check -> cheater sample
    from-punishments: true
    # Player online {clean-play-minutes} minutes with 0 violations -> legit sample
    from-clean-play: true
    clean-play-minutes: 20
    # How many legit samples one clean player may donate per session
    max-clean-samples: 3

  # AI probability above which staff get a dedicated AI alert
  alert-threshold: 0.90
  # AI adjusts per-player check sensitivity (thresholds shrink for suspicious players)
  adaptive-thresholds: true
  # multiplier = clamp(high - (high-low)*probability, low, high)
  adaptive-min-multiplier: 0.65
  adaptive-max-multiplier: 1.30
  # EXPERIMENTAL: let AI alone trigger punishments at very high probability.
  # Keep false until the model has trained on your server.
  punish-by-ai: false
  punish-probability: 0.995

  # Learning from real PvP: watch honest players fight and teach the model.
  # A PvP session (player hits player) donates a LEGIT-labelled telemetry
  # snapshot when it ends, but only if the donor passes all gates:
  #   - zero active violations, police record CLEAN,
  #   - AI probability below max-ai-probability,
  #   - session was a real fight (duration + hit count).
  # If a donor is later punished, ALL their past donations are purged.
  pvp-learning:
    enabled: true
    session-timeout-seconds: 10   # no PvP hits for this long -> session over
    min-session-seconds: 15       # minimum fight duration to learn from
    min-hits: 20                  # minimum exchanged hits
    max-ai-probability: 0.35      # donors the AI already suspects are skipped
    max-samples-per-hour: 3       # donation rate limit per player

# ------------------------------------------------------------
#  CHECKS
#  threshold = VL needed before punishment stages apply
# ------------------------------------------------------------
checks:
  fly:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 20
    # Ticks airborne while NOT losing altitude (hovering/rising)
    max-hover-ticks: 8
    # Flag client "on ground" while no solid block below (ground spoof)
    detect-ground-spoof: true
    # Physics simulation: accumulated blocks of "extra height" vs gravity
    # simulation before flagging (catches glide/slow-fall/gradual-climb fly)
    physics-divergence: 0.75
  speed:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 25
    # Max allowed = vanilla_limit * multiplier (vanilla sprint-jump ~0.29 b/tick)
    multiplier: 1.45
    buffer: 8
  jesus:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    # Ticks walking on water surface before flag
    max-ticks: 12
  nofall:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Minimum fall distance (blocks) to require fall damage
    min-fall-distance: 4.0
  fastclimb:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    multiplier: 1.6
  wallclimb:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Minimum sustained upward speed (blocks/tick) while touching a wall
    min-rise: 0.03
    # Height climbed above the wall-contact point before flagging.
    # A vanilla jump along a wall rises at most ~1.25 blocks.
    min-climb-height: 1.3
  airjump:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Sudden upward velocity spikes while airborne (double jump)
    buffer: 2
  phase:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 10
    # Consecutive ticks fully inside solid occluding cubes before flagging
    min-ticks: 30
  reach:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Max eye-to-hitbox distance in blocks
    max-reach: 3.05
    # Add ping/1000 * compensation blocks (capped)
    ping-compensation: 0.35
    max-ping-compensation: 0.6
  autoclicker:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 20
    # 1.9+ reality: damage is gated by the attack cooldown, and legit
    # butterfly/drag clickers reach 20+ CPS. The CPS cap only catches
    # impossible spam; the real signal is robotic interval consistency.
    max-cps: 25
    # Robotic consistency: std deviation of click intervals below this (ms)
    # over at least {min-samples} clicks -> flag
    min-interval-stddev: 12.0
    min-samples: 45
  killaura:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 18
    # A. Max attacks per rolling second
    max-attacks-per-second: 14
    # B. Max angle (degrees) between look direction and a PLAYER victim
    max-rotation-angle: 75.0
    # C. Snap-aim: whole rotation onto the victim within one packet burst
    snap-aim: true
    snap-new-angle: 10.0      # aim lands within this many degrees of the victim
    snap-old-angle: 32.0      # previous rotation was this far off the victim
    snap-window-ms: 200       # rotation finished at most this long before the hit
    # D. Mouse GCD: real mice quantise pitch deltas to a sensitivity constant
    gcd-rotations: true
    gcd-min: 0.005
    # E. Pacing: metronome-regular attack intervals
    attack-pacing: true
    pacing-min-stddev: 25.0
    # F. NoSwing: melee damage without an arm swing
    no-swing: true
    swing-window-ms: 400
    # G. Aim-lock: crosshair glued to the victim with near-zero variance
    aim-lock: true
    lock-max-stddev: 0.5
    # H. Multi-target: many distinct victims in a short window, all on-view
    multi-target: true
    multi-max-targets: 4
    multi-window-ms: 1500
    multi-max-angle: 40.0
    # I. PerfectHits: aim-gated auras (vesence "Shard" etc.) attack only when the
    #    crosshair settled on the target, so nearly every PvP hit lands within
    #    ~1-2 degrees. Humans time clicks, their hit-angle spread is much wider.
    perfect-hits: true
    perfect-window: 12        # PvP hits analysed
    perfect-angle: 2.5        # degrees from crosshair counted as "perfect"
    perfect-ratio: 0.8        # fraction of perfect hits required to flag
    # J. CooldownLock (1.9+ meta): the aura fires the instant the weapon reaches
    #    full charge - charge pinned at ~1.0 with near-zero variance across many
    #    hits AND metronomic intervals. Humans swing early/late and miss rhythm.
    cooldown-lock: true
    lock-min-attacks: 15
    lock-min-charge: 0.98
    lock-max-charge-stddev: 0.012
    lock-max-interval-stddev: 80
    # D2. Yaw GCD: horizontal-only rotators evade the pitch-GCD test
    gcd-yaw: true
    # K. GUI attacks: attacking with a container open is impossible in vanilla
    gui-attacks: true
    # Isolated suspicious events farther apart than this do NOT accumulate into a
    # flag (only sustained bursts confirm). Prevents slow noise from "ripening".
    buffer-reset-gap-ms: 4000
  fastbow:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 10
    # Full bow charge takes ~1100ms in vanilla
    min-charge-ms: 300
    min-force: 0.75
  fastbreak:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    # Minimum ms between block breaks for non-instant blocks
    min-interval-ms: 90
  fastplace:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    min-interval-ms: 70
  scaffold:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    # Flag placing blocks below feet while looking forward (pitch below this)
    max-pitch: 25.0
    buffer: 4
  fastregen:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 10
    # Minimum ms between natural saturation regen events
    min-interval-ms: 3000
  fasteat:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 10
    # Vanilla eating/drinking takes ~1600ms (dried kelp is excluded, it is 400ms)
    min-consume-ms: 900
  step:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    # Rising faster than the 0.6-block auto-step while grounded
    max-rise: 0.7
    buffer: 3
  noslow:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    # Vanilla slows to ~20% speed while using items (~0.05 b/t)
    max-speed: 0.12
    buffer: 6
  waterwalk:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 15
    # Fast swim/water-speed hacks (+0.07 per Depth Strider level is allowed)
    max-speed: 0.32
    buffer: 6
  elytrafly:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Climbing while gliding without fireworks/riptide for N samples (4Hz)
    max-rise: 0.08
    samples: 5
    # Unboosted elytra terminal velocity is ~2.5-2.7 b/t
    max-speed: 3.2
  boatfly:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 3
    # Speed caps per surface (blocks/tick). Rowing ~0.4, ice roads up to ~1.6.
    ice-speed-cap: 1.8
    water-speed-cap: 0.55
    land-speed-cap: 0.35
    # Consecutive VehicleMoveEvents before each signal flags
    hover-events: 6
    rise-events: 4
    speed-events: 8
  noweb:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Vanilla crawls at ~0.05 b/t inside cobweb / berry bushes
    web-max-speed: 0.20
    berry-max-speed: 0.25
    buffer: 4
  inventorymove:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Camera rotation while a GUI is open is impossible in vanilla
    min-rotation: 2.0
    buffer: 5
  cheststealer:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Human shift-click spam tops out around 8-10 clicks/sec
    max-clicks-per-second: 14
    window-ms: 1200
    buffer: 2
  airstuck:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 10
    # Frozen mid-air for this many ticks
    max-ticks: 60
  longjump:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Horizontal bursts mid-air beyond sprint-jump physics (~0.35 b/t)
    max-air-speed: 0.55
    buffer: 2
  timer:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 8
  blink:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 8
  badpackets:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 5
  criticals:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    buffer: 3
    # Crit rhythm (1.9+): jumping before every attack is legit, but a bot does
    # it perfectly - 95%+ of PvP hits airborne at a metronomic interval.
    crit-rhythm: true
    rhythm-min-attacks: 15
    rhythm-air-ratio: 0.95
    rhythm-max-interval-stddev: 60
  velocity:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Anti-KB ("Velocity" modules, e.g. CatLean's flagship absorbing 85-90%):
    # after a significant hit the player must travel at least this fraction
    # of the expected knockback distance
    absorb-ratio: 0.35
    buffer: 2
  silentrot:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
    # Silent rotations: server-side pitch snaps down for an action, then back
    # to the camera angle - large fast reversals no human tracking sustains.
    # Thresholds are deliberately high: legit players DO flick up/down in fights,
    # so we require several LARGE reversals in a short window before flagging.
    min-swing-deg: 30.0     # magnitude of each side of a reversal
    flip-window-ms: 500     # max time between the two sides of one reversal
    window-ms: 5000         # rolling window to count reversals in
    min-reversals: 5
    buffer: 2
  xray:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 10
    # Statistical ore find-rate. Legit branch mining: ~0.5-1.5 diamond-tier
    # ores per 1000 stone. Xray walks straight to ores: 10-100x that.
    # Conservative by design: 30-min window, high minimums, alerts first.
    window-ms: 1800000
    min-stone: 800
    min-valuables: 20
    max-ratio-per-1000: 12.0
  groundspoof:
    enabled: true
    threshold: 12
```

```java
import org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;

import java.io.File;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/** Offline verification of the FIXED punishment-stage parsing. */
public class ConfigProbe {
    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        YamlConfiguration c = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(
                new File("src/main/resources/config.yml"));

        System.out.println("=== punishments.default-stages (FIXED: getMapList from root) ===");
        List<Map<?, ?>> def = c.getMapList("punishments.default-stages");
        System.out.println("size = " + def.size());
        for (Map<?, ?> m : def) System.out.println("  at=" + m.get("at") + " cmd='" + m.get("command") + "'");

        System.out.println("=== punishments.overrides.BOATFLY (FIXED) ===");
        ConfigurationSection ov = c.getConfigurationSection("punishments.overrides");
        if (ov != null) {
            for (String key : ov.getKeys(false)) {
                List<Map<?, ?>> list = c.getMapList("punishments.overrides." + key);
                System.out.println("  override " + key + " size=" + list.size());
                for (Map<?, ?> m : list) System.out.println("    at=" + m.get("at") + " cmd='" + m.get("command") + "'");
            }
        } else {
            System.out.println("  overrides section NULL");
        }

        System.out.println("=== configKey() check (GROUND_SPOOF must map to existing section) ===");
        String key = "GROUND_SPOOF".toLowerCase().replace("_", "");
        System.out.println("  derived key = '" + key + "', section present = "
                + (c.getConfigurationSection("checks." + key) != null)
                + ", enabled = " + c.getBoolean("checks." + key + ".enabled", true)
                + ", threshold = " + c.getInt("checks." + key + ".threshold", 20));

        System.out.println("=== killaura new options present ===");
        for (String k : new String[]{"gcd-yaw", "gui-attacks", "cooldown-lock", "buffer-reset-gap-ms", "perfect-hits"}) {
            System.out.println("  killaura." + k + " = " + c.get("checks.killaura." + k));
        }
        System.out.println("=== silentrot tuned ===");
        System.out.println("  min-reversals=" + c.getDouble("checks.silentrot.min-reversals")
                + " min-swing-deg=" + c.getDouble("checks.silentrot.min-swing-deg"));
    }
}
```

`tools/NNSmokeTest.java`:

```java
import dev.weposs.wepac.ai.NeuralNetwork;

import java.util.Random;

/**
 * Standalone smoke test: can the WepAC network actually learn to separate
 * cheater-like telemetry from legit telemetry? Run:
 *   javac -cp target/classes -d tools tools/NNSmokeTest.java
 *   java  -cp target/classes;tools NNSmokeTest
 */
public class NNSmokeTest {

    static final int DIMS = 14;
    static final Random R = new Random(7);

    // population "normaliser" like AIBrain uses
    static final double[] mean = new double[DIMS];
    static final double[] std = new double[DIMS];

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        double[][] train = new double[400][];
        double[] labels = new double[400];
        for (int i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
            boolean cheater = i % 2 == 0;
            train[i] = cheater ? cheaterSample() : legitSample();
            labels[i] = cheater ? 1.0 : 0.0;
        }

        // learn population stats
        long n = 0;
        for (double[] f : train) {
            n++;
            for (int j = 0; j < DIMS; j++) {
                double d = f[j] - mean[j];
                mean[j] += d / n;
                double d2 = f[j] - mean[j];
                std[j] = n < 2 ? 1 : Math.sqrt((std[j] * std[j] * (n - 2) + d * d2) / (n - 1));
            }
        }

        NeuralNetwork net = new NeuralNetwork(DIMS, 16, 8, 1);
        net.setLearningRate(0.02);

        // online mini-batch training like AIBrain.trainRound()
        for (int round = 0; round < 300; round++) {
            for (int b = 0; b < 32; b++) {
                int idx = R.nextInt(train.length);
                net.train(normalize(train[idx]), labels[idx]);
            }
        }

        // evaluate on fresh unseen samples
        int correct = 0, total = 200;
        double errSum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < total; i++) {
            boolean cheater = i % 2 == 0;
            double[] f = cheater ? cheaterSample() : legitSample();
            double p = net.predict(normalize(f));
            errSum += Math.abs(p - (cheater ? 1.0 : 0.0));
            if ((p > 0.5) == cheater) correct++;
        }
        System.out.printf("accuracy=%.1f%%  avgErr=%.3f  trainSteps=%d%n",
                100.0 * correct / total, errSum / total, net.trainSteps());
        if (correct < total * 0.9) {
            System.out.println("FAIL: network did not learn separation");
            System.exit(1);
        }
        System.out.println("OK: network learned to separate cheater/legit telemetry");
    }

    static double[] normalize(double[] raw) {
        double[] out = new double[DIMS];
        for (int i = 0; i < DIMS; i++) {
            out[i] = Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, (raw[i] - mean[i]) / Math.max(std[i], 1e-6)));
        }
        return out;
    }

    // feature order: cps, clickStd, clickMin, speedAvg, speedMax, airRatio,
    //               riseRatio, aps, reachAvg, breakMs, placeMs, yawRate, pitchRate, ping
    static double[] legitSample() {
        return new double[]{
                4 + R.nextGaussian() * 1.5,      // cps
                60 + R.nextGaussian() * 25,      // click stddev ms
                55 + R.nextGaussian() * 20,      // min interval
                0.15 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.03,  // speed avg
                0.28 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.05,  // speed max
                0.25 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.08,  // air ratio
                0.35 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.15,  // rise ratio
                0.8 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.3,    // aps
                2.1 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.3,    // reach avg
                0.35 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.1,   // break s
                0.30 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.1,   // place s
                8 + R.nextGaussian() * 4,        // yaw rate
                5 + R.nextGaussian() * 3,        // pitch rate
                0.6 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.2     // ping/100
        };
    }

    static double[] cheaterSample() {
        return new double[]{
                16 + R.nextGaussian() * 1.0,     // cps
                8 + R.nextGaussian() * 2,        // robotic consistency
                45 + R.nextGaussian() * 3,
                0.34 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.02,  // speed hacks
                0.60 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.05,
                0.75 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.1,   // fly: mostly airborne
                0.70 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.1,   // rising/hovering
                10 + R.nextGaussian() * 1.5,     // aura: attacks/sec
                4.3 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.4,    // reach
                0.06 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.01,  // fastbreak
                0.05 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.01,  // fastplace
                45 + R.nextGaussian() * 15,      // snappy rotations
                30 + R.nextGaussian() * 10,
                0.6 + R.nextGaussian() * 0.2
        };
    }
}
```

`tools/PreTrainer.java`:

```java
import dev.weposs.wepac.ai.NeuralNetwork;

import java.io.BufferedOutputStream;
import java.io.DataOutputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Random;

/**
 * Offline pre-trainer for the WepAC AI, v2.
 *
 * Archetypes are derived from REAL cheat client parameters (vesence: cps 15,
 * attackRange 3.0-6.0, Shard settle gate 8.5 deg, GCD-snapped rotations,
 * Neuro/Spooky/Snap rotation modes, 1.8-CPS tick-quantised pacing) plus the
 * generic cheat and honest-player archetypes.
 *
 * Feature order (FeatureVector v15):
 *  0 cps, 1 clickStd(ms), 2 clickMin(ms), 3 speedAvg(b/t), 4 speedMax(b/t),
 *  5 airRatio, 6 riseRatio, 7 aps, 8 reachAvg, 9 breakSec, 10 placeSec,
 *  11 yawRate(deg), 12 pitchRate(deg), 13 ping/100, 14 hitAngleAvg(deg)
 *
 * Run:
 *   javac -cp target/classes -d tools tools/PreTrainer.java
 *   java  -cp "target/classes;tools" PreTrainer
 *   -> pretrained/model.bin  (copy to plugins/WepAC/ai/model.bin)
 */
public class PreTrainer {

    static final int DIMS = 18;
    static final int MAGIC = 0x57455041; // must match AIBrain
    static final Random R = new Random(1337);

    record Archetype(String name, boolean cheat, double[] mean, double[] sd) {}

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        List<Archetype> archetypes = buildArchetypes();
        List<double[]> features = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Double> labels = new ArrayList<>();
        List<String> origins = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Archetype a : archetypes) {
            int n = a.cheat() ? 220 : 500;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                features.add(sample(a));
                labels.add(a.cheat() ? 1.0 : 0.0);
                origins.add(a.name());
            }
        }
        System.out.printf("Generated %d samples (%d archetypes)%n", features.size(), archetypes.size());

        // population normaliser (identical Welford update to AIBrain.observe)
        double[] mean = new double[DIMS];
        double[] std = new double[DIMS];
        long normCount = 0;
        for (double[] f : features) {
            normCount++;
            for (int j = 0; j < DIMS; j++) {
                double d = f[j] - mean[j];
                mean[j] += d / normCount;
                double d2 = f[j] - mean[j];
                std[j] = normCount < 2 ? 1.0
                        : Math.sqrt((std[j] * std[j] * (normCount - 2) + d * d2) / (normCount - 1));
            }
        }

        // shuffle + 85/15 split
        List<Integer> order = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < features.size(); i++) order.add(i);
        Collections.shuffle(order, R);
        int split = (int) (order.size() * 0.85);
        List<Integer> trainIdx = order.subList(0, split);
        List<Integer> testIdx = order.subList(split, order.size());

        NeuralNetwork net = new NeuralNetwork(DIMS, 32, 16, 1);
        net.setLearningRate(0.02);

        int rounds = 18000;
        for (int round = 1; round <= rounds; round++) {
            for (int b = 0; b < 32; b++) {
                int idx = trainIdx.get(R.nextInt(trainIdx.size()));
                net.train(normalize(features.get(idx), mean, std), labels.get(idx));
            }
            if (round == 4000) net.setLearningRate(0.008);
            if (round == 7500) net.setLearningRate(0.003);
            if (round == 14000) net.setLearningRate(0.0012);
            if (round % 1000 == 0) {
                double acc = accuracy(net, features, labels, testIdx, mean, std);
                System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "round %4d: test accuracy %.2f%%%n", round, acc * 100);
            }
        }

        report(net, features, labels, origins, testIdx, mean, std);

        // save model.bin in the exact AIBrain format
        File out = new File("pretrained/model.bin");
        out.getParentFile().mkdirs();
        List<Integer> shuffledAll = new ArrayList<>(order);
        Collections.shuffle(shuffledAll, R);
        int bufCap = Math.min(5000, shuffledAll.size());
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        try (DataOutputStream dos = new DataOutputStream(
                new BufferedOutputStream(new FileOutputStream(out)))) {
            dos.writeInt(MAGIC);
            net.save(dos);
            dos.writeLong(normCount);
            for (double v : mean) dos.writeDouble(v);
            for (double v : std) dos.writeDouble(v);
            dos.writeInt(bufCap);
            for (int i = 0; i < bufCap; i++) {
                int idx = shuffledAll.get(i);
                double[] f = features.get(idx);
                for (int j = 0; j < DIMS; j++) dos.writeDouble(f[j]);
                dos.writeDouble(labels.get(idx));
                dos.writeLong(now - R.nextInt(86_400_000));
            }
        }
        System.out.printf("Saved %s (%.1f KB, buffer %d samples, trainSteps %d)%n",
                out.getPath(), out.length() / 1024.0, bufCap, net.trainSteps());
    }

    static double[] normalize(double[] raw, double[] mean, double[] std) {
        double[] out = new double[DIMS];
        for (int i = 0; i < DIMS; i++) {
            out[i] = Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, (raw[i] - mean[i]) / Math.max(std[i], 1e-6)));
        }
        return out;
    }

    static double[] sample(Archetype a) {
        double[] f = new double[DIMS];
        for (int i = 0; i < DIMS; i++) {
            f[i] = Math.max(0, a.mean()[i] + R.nextGaussian() * a.sd()[i]);
        }
        for (int i : new int[]{5, 6}) f[i] = Math.min(1.0, f[i]);
        f[2] = Math.max(5, f[2]);
        return f;
    }

    static double accuracy(NeuralNetwork net, List<double[]> f, List<Double> l,
                           List<Integer> idx, double[] mean, double[] std) {
        int ok = 0;
        for (int i : idx) {
            double p = net.predict(normalize(f.get(i), mean, std));
            if ((p > 0.5) == (l.get(i) > 0.5)) ok++;
        }
        return (double) ok / idx.size();
    }

    static void report(NeuralNetwork net, List<double[]> features, List<Double> labels,
                       List<String> origins, List<Integer> testIdx, double[] mean, double[] std) {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String o : origins) if (!names.contains(o)) names.add(o);

        System.out.println("\n=== per-archetype results (test set) ===");
        double worstCheat = 1.0, worstLegit = 1.0;
        for (String name : names) {
            int total = 0, correct = 0;
            double sum = 0;
            boolean cheat = false;
            for (int i : testIdx) {
                if (!origins.get(i).equals(name)) continue;
                total++;
                double p = net.predict(normalize(features.get(i), mean, std));
                cheat = labels.get(i) > 0.5;
                sum += p;
                if ((p > 0.5) == cheat) correct++;
            }
            if (total == 0) continue;
            double rate = (double) correct / total;
            if (cheat) worstCheat = Math.min(worstCheat, rate);
            else worstLegit = Math.min(worstLegit, rate);
            System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%-22s %-7s %5.1f%%  avgP=%.3f%n",
                    name, cheat ? "CHEAT" : "legit", rate * 100, sum / total);
        }
        System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%nWORST cheat detection: %.1f%% | WORST legit protection: %.1f%%%n",
                worstCheat * 100, worstLegit * 100);
    }

    // ---------------- 1.9+ combat features (15: chargeAvg, 16: airAttackRatio) -------------

    static final java.util.Set<String> CRIT_AURAS = java.util.Set.of(
            "toshiba-silent", "minced-builder", "zenith-aura");

    static final java.util.Set<String> CD_AURAS = java.util.Set.of(
            "vesence-neuro", "vesence-shard", "stalin-neuro", "evaware-smooth", "wild-ai",
            "meow-neuro", "arbuz-onnx", "health-ml", "keyz-aimassist", "xrose-aura",
            "zov-bezier", "timoha-aura", "phobia-smooth", "winlend-wonderful",
            "exploit-aura", "cometa-aura", "aura-smooth", "velka-ml", "obsidian-punch",
            "monoton-aura", "nursultan-aura", "wild-aura", "minced-grim",
            "catlean-aura", "catlean-reach", "reach", "triggerbot");

    static final java.util.Set<String> SPAM_AURAS = java.util.Set.of(
            "vesence-spooky", "vesence-snap", "crystall-aura", "delta-aura", "essence-aura",
            "euphoria-aura", "wave-aura", "sadnes-snap", "skycore-snap", "wexside-hvh",
            "arbuz-aura", "aura-aggressive", "autoclicker", "hvh-mix", "exploit-snap",
            "essence-pattern");

    static final java.util.Map<String, double[]> LEGIT_COMBAT = java.util.Map.of(
            "pvper", new double[]{.88, .10, .50, .20},
            "jitter-kid", new double[]{.50, .20, .30, .15},
            "casual", new double[]{.65, .20, .15, .10},
            "builder", new double[]{.60, .20, .10, .10},
            "miner", new double[]{.60, .20, .10, .10},
            "parkour", new double[]{.70, .15, .35, .15},
            "laggy", new double[]{.60, .20, .30, .15},
            "elytra", new double[]{.60, .20, .20, .15});

    // server-visible silent rotation oscillators (pitch snap down/up per action)
    static final java.util.Set<String> SILENT_ROT = java.util.Set.of(
            "scaffold", "catlean-scaffold");
    static final java.util.Set<String> SILENT_SOME = java.util.Set.of(
            "vesence-spooky", "minced-builder", "health-ml", "arbuz-onnx",
            "wild-ai", "winlend-wonderful");

    /** Pads archetypes to 18 dims (15/16: combat discipline, 17: silent rotations). */
    static List<Archetype> extendCombat(List<Archetype> base) {
        List<Archetype> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Archetype a : base) {
            if (a.mean().length >= DIMS) {
                out.add(a);
                continue;
            }
            double[] m = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(a.mean(), DIMS);
            double[] s = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(a.sd(), DIMS);
            String n = a.name();

            // features 15/16 - honour explicit values if the archetype has them
            if (a.mean().length <= 15) {
                double[] legit = LEGIT_COMBAT.get(n);
                if (legit != null) {
                    m[15] = legit[0]; s[15] = legit[1];
                    m[16] = legit[2]; s[16] = legit[3];
                } else if (CRIT_AURAS.contains(n)) {
                    m[15] = .99; s[15] = .01;
                    m[16] = .96; s[16] = .03;
                } else if (CD_AURAS.contains(n)) {
                    m[15] = .98; s[15] = .02;
                    m[16] = .48; s[16] = .20;
                } else if (SPAM_AURAS.contains(n)) {
                    m[15] = .40; s[15] = .15;
                    m[16] = .35; s[16] = .15;
                } else if (a.cheat()) {
                    m[15] = .85; s[15] = .10;
                    m[16] = .30; s[16] = .15;
                } else {
                    m[15] = .75; s[15] = .15;
                    m[16] = .20; s[16] = .12;
                }
            }

            // feature 17 - pitch reversal rate (silent rotations)
            if (SILENT_ROT.contains(n)) {
                m[17] = 3.0; s[17] = 1.0;
            } else if (SILENT_SOME.contains(n)) {
                m[17] = 1.5; s[17] = 0.8;
            } else if (a.cheat()) {
                m[17] = 0.5; s[17] = 0.4;
            } else if (n.equals("pvper") || n.equals("jitter-kid")) {
                m[17] = 0.5; s[17] = 0.35;
            } else {
                m[17] = 0.2; s[17] = 0.15;
            }

            out.add(new Archetype(n, a.cheat(), m, s));
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```
